📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 2025-2026 年流感季节,来自美日的 H3N2 K 谱系在单个季节内取代 J.2 亚谱系,并展开以美国为中心的非典型全球传播模式。
  • H3N2 K 谱系在一个流感季节内迅速取代之前共传播的 J.2 亚谱系,其扩散中心位于美国而非传统的东亚东南亚源系统[10.1016/j.ijid.2026.109188]。
  • K 谱系定义性突变最早在美国和日本被检测到,累积多抗原位点替入但 HA 结构稳定性仍得以保留[10.1016/j.ijid.2026.109188]。
  • mRNA 疫苗 FLUm3HA-3NA 和 FLUm4HA-4NA 诱导 HA 与 NA 双靶抗体及中和反应,安全性良好,支持进一步开发[10.1007/s40121-026-01462-w]。
  • 泰国前哨监测显示感染 H3N2 亚分支 K 的住院风险与其他流感病毒无显著差异(aOR 1.06)[10.1093/infdis/jiag283]。
  • During the 2025-2026 influenza season, the H3N2 K lineage originating from the United States and Japan rapidly replaced co-circulating J.2 sublineages within a single season and spread globally via a non-canonical, US-centered pattern.
  • The H3N2 K lineage rapidly replaced previously co-circulating J.2 sublineages within a single influenza season, with global expansion centered on the United States rather than the traditional East-Southeast Asia source system [10.1016/j.ijid.2026.109188].
  • K-lineage-defining substitutions were first detected in the United States and Japan; despite accumulating multiple antigenic-site substitutions, overall HA structural stability was preserved [10.1016/j.ijid.2026.109188].
  • mRNA vaccines FLUm3HA-3NA and FLUm4HA-4NA induced hemagglutination inhibition, neuraminidase inhibition, and neutralizing responses against multiple strains with acceptable safety profiles, supporting further development [10.1007/s40121-026-01462-w].
  • Sentinel surveillance in Thailand found no significant difference in hospitalization risk for H3N2 subclade K infections versus other influenza viruses (adjusted OR 1.06) [10.1093/infdis/jiag283].

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期季节性流感病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 流感活动保持稳定,处于基线水平,一些指标显示年轻成年人中的活动有所下降。流感样病例(ILI)的急诊科(ED)就诊率保持稳定,并高于季节性预期水平。社区中确认为流感的急性呼吸道感染(ARI)事件数量较前一周有所减少。
  • 美国食品药品监督管理局基于临床试验结果批准了Moderna的mRNA季节性流感疫苗疫苗,该疫苗专为50岁以上成年人设计,结果显示与当前标准疫苗疫苗相比,确诊流感病例减少了27%(N10). [news-9d586e3bcbe48801]。
  • 基因组测序在布隆迪一所学校中识别出流感甲型H3N2流感病毒亚系K的暴发,最初疑似由SARS-CoV-2引起,但通过检测被排除。 [news-36af7d82bc6635b6]。
  • 该报告讨论了季节性H1N1和H3N2流感病毒中出现的抗病毒药物抗药性标记,特别是H1N1中的I223V和S247N 突变,以及H3N2中的E119V 突变。这些突变会降低对奥司他韦和帕拉米韦的敏感性。这些发现基于发表在《柳叶刀·微生物》、《EID期刊》、《病毒研究》和《欧洲流行病学监测》上的研究。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Seasonal influenza viruses news developments.
  • Influenza activity remained stable and is circulating at baseline levels, some indicators showed decreased activity among young adults.
  • The FDA approved Moderna's mRNA seasonal flu vaccine, which is designed for adults over 50, based on clinical trial results showing a 27% reduction in confirmed flu cases compared to the current standard vaccine (N10).
  • Genomic sequencing identified an outbreak of Influenza A(H3N2) subclade K in a Burundian school, initially suspected to be caused by SARS-CoV-2 but later ruled out through testing. [news-36af7d82bc6635b6].
  • The report discusses the emergence of antiviral resistance markers in seasonal H1N1 and H3N2 influenza viruses, specifically the I223V and S247N mutations in H1N1, and the E119V mutation in H3N2.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Infectious Diseases and Therapy  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-15  |  DOI: 10.1007/s40121-026-01462-w  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

评估基于 mRNA 的 季节性流感 疫苗 候选编码血凝素和神经氨酸酶的免疫原性和安全性的 2 期试验。

A Phase 2 Trial Evaluating the Immunogenicity and Safety of an mRNA-Based Seasonal Influenza Vaccine Candidate Encoding Hemagglutinin and Neuraminidase.
作者： Brian Moldt, Rafael A. Ubeda, Alexander V. Murray, Giresse Tima, Sophie Germain, Clarisse Lorin, Leroy Jide Ovbude, Danaé Steinbrecher, Marcus Nordgren, FLU SV mRNA-024 study group
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 季节性流感 病毒中针对血凝素 (HA) 和神经氨酸酶 (NA) 的基于 mRNA 的 疫苗 候选药物的免疫原性和安全性,因为目前的 疫苗 主要针对 HA,可能无法提供广泛的保护。
设计与对象
一项随机 2 期试验在两个年龄段的 队列研究 中进行:年轻人(18-64 岁)和老年人(65-85 岁)。参与者接受 mRNA 疫苗 制剂或许可的 季节性流感 疫苗 作为比较剂。
核心方法
该研究将第 29 天的血凝抑制 (HI) 和神经氨酸酶抑制 (NI) 反应分别评估为主要和次要终点,并以病毒中和作为第三终点。安全性和反应原性也作为次要终点进行评估。
主要结果
在第 1 部分中,两个年龄组中,FLUm3HA-3NA 后针对 A/H1N1 和 A/H3N2 的 HI 反应与比较剂 疫苗接种 后相似或更高。对于 B/维多利亚州,年轻人的 HI 反应较低或相似,老年人的 HI 反应相似或较高。在第 2 部分中,在两个年龄组中,FLUm3HA-3NA 的 HI 反应与 A/H1N1 和 A/H3N2 的对照剂相似或更高,而 B/Victoria 的 HI 反应较低。与比较株相比,使用 FLUm3HA-3NA 的所有三种毒株的 NI 效价均较高,A/H1N1 和 A/H3N2 的中和效价相似或更高,B/Victoria 的中和效价相似或更高。
解释与创新
作者解释说,疫苗 诱导 免疫应答 产生 HA 和 NA,具有可接受的安全性,并指出结果支持进一步开发,特别是侧重于改进 免疫应答 对 B 型流感病毒的作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对 mRNA 疫苗 中的 HA 和 NA 可以增强对 季节性流感 病毒的保护,可能会产生更有效的 疫苗,从而解决当前仅包含 HA 的 疫苗 的局限性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于两个年龄组,没有评估长期疗效或免疫持久性。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于免疫原性和安全性评估,没有长期随访或更广泛的人群数据。

A Phase 2 Trial Evaluating the Immunogenicity and Safety of an mRNA-Based Seasonal Influenza Vaccine Candidate Encoding Hemagglutinin and Neuraminidase.

Authors: Brian Moldt, Rafael A. Ubeda, Alexander V. Murray, Giresse Tima, Sophie Germain, Clarisse Lorin, Leroy Jide Ovbude, Danaé Steinbrecher, Marcus Nordgren, FLU SV mRNA-024 study group
Original abstract (English)
Influenza vaccines targeting both hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) might offer broader protection than current vaccines, which primarily target HA. This randomized phase 2 study evaluated the immunogenicity and safety of an investigational mRNA-based trivalent seasonal influenza vaccine encoding HA and NA from A/H1N1, A/H3N2, and B/Victoria (FLUm3HA-3NA). Participants aged 18–64 years (younger adults) or 65–85 years (older adults) received one of several mRNA vaccine formulations—each containing different influenza A and B HA mRNA doses—or an age-appropriate licensed seasonal influenza vaccine as a comparator. Four formulations per age cohort were tested in part 1 of the study, and one formulation per age cohort in part 2. Day-29 hemagglutination inhibition (HI) and NA inhibition (NI) responses were assessed as primary and secondary endpoints, respectively, safety/reactogenicity as the secondary, and virus neutralization as the tertiary endpoint. A total of 250 younger and 260 older adults were vaccinated in part 1; 161 and 162, respectively, in part 2. All FLUm3HA-3NA formulations induced HI, NI, and neutralizing responses above baseline. In part 1, HI responses against A/H1N1 and A/H3N2 were similar or higher after FLUm3HA-3NA than after comparator vaccination in both age groups. For B/Victoria, HI responses were lower or similar versus comparator vaccination in younger adults and similar or higher in older adults. In part 2, HI responses were similar or higher with FLUm3HA-3NA versus comparators for A/H1N1 and A/H3N2, and lower for B/Victoria in both age groups. In both study parts and both age groups, NI titers were higher for all three strains with FLUm3HA-3NA versus comparators, and neutralizing titers were similar or higher for A/H1N1 and A/H3N2 and similar for B/Victoria. No safety concerns were identified. FLUm3HA-3NA induced HI, NI, and neutralizing responses, with an acceptable safety profile. The results support further development, including optimization of the influenza B HI response. ClinicalTrials.gov: NCT06431607. Each year, seasonal flu A and flu B viruses infect about 1 billion people worldwide, causing up to 650,000 deaths. Yearly vaccination helps protect against flu. Because flu viruses change over time, vaccines are not always fully effective. New vaccine technologies, including mRNA-based vaccines, are being explored to broaden protection. The available flu vaccines primarily target one key virus protein, called hemagglutinin (HA). Targeting a second virus protein, neuraminidase (NA), could improve protection. We therefore tested an mRNA vaccine candidate designed to target HA and NA from flu A and B viruses. The study evaluated formulations with different flu A and B HA mRNA amounts. The study analyzed 411 adults aged 18–64 years and 422 aged 65–85 years. Participants received either an mRNA vaccine formulation or a licensed flu vaccine as the comparator. The mRNA vaccine induced immune responses to both NA and HA. The NA response was higher with the mRNA vaccine than with the licensed vaccines for the flu A and B viruses. The HA response to the flu A viruses was similar or higher with the mRNA vaccine than with the licensed vaccines. The HA response to the flu B virus was lower or similar with the mRNA vaccine. No safety issues were identified. These results encourage further clinical evaluation of this mRNA HA/NA vaccine, focusing on strengthening the immune response to the flu B virus.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the immunogenicity and safety of an mRNA-based vaccine candidate targeting both hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) in seasonal influenza viruses, as current vaccines primarily target HA and may not provide broad protection.
Design and population
A randomized phase 2 trial was conducted in two age cohorts: younger adults (18–64 years) and older adults (65–85 years). Participants received either an mRNA vaccine formulation or a licensed seasonal influenza vaccine as the comparator.
Core methods
The study assessed Day-29 hemagglutination inhibition (HI) and neuraminidase inhibition (NI) responses as primary and secondary endpoints, respectively, with virus neutralization as the tertiary endpoint. Safety and reactogenicity were also evaluated as secondary endpoints.
Main results
In part 1, HI responses against A/H1N1 and A/H3N2 were similar or higher after FLUm3HA-3NA than after comparator vaccination in both age groups. For B/Victoria, HI responses were lower or similar in younger adults and similar or higher in older adults. In part 2, HI responses were similar or higher with FLUm3HA-3NA versus comparators for A/H1N1 and A/H3N2, and lower for B/Victoria in both age groups. NI titers were higher for all three strains with FLUm3HA-3NA versus comparators, and neutralizing titers were similar or higher for A/H1N1 and A/H3N2 and similar for B/Victoria.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the vaccine induced immune responses to both HA and NA, with an acceptable safety profile, and noted that the results support further development, particularly focusing on improving the immune response to the flu B virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeting both HA and NA in an mRNA vaccine could enhance protection against seasonal influenza viruses, potentially leading to more effective vaccines that address the limitations of current HA-only vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to two age groups and did not evaluate long-term efficacy or immune persistence. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on immunogenicity and safety assessments without long-term follow-up or broader population data.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

评估基于 mRNA 的 季节性流感 疫苗 候选编码血凝素和神经氨酸酶的安全性和免疫原性的 1/2 期试验。

A Phase 1/2 Trial Evaluating the Safety and Immunogenicity of an mRNA-based Seasonal Influenza Vaccine Candidate Encoding Hemagglutinin and Neuraminidase.
作者： Ilse De Coster, Jose F. Cardona, Murdo Ferguson, Isabel Leroux-Roels, Haylene Nell, Stefano Berrè, Sonia Schoonbroodt, Clarisse Lorin, Leroy Jide Ovbude, Brian Moldt
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估编码血凝素和神经氨酸酶蛋白的基于 mRNA 的 季节性流感 疫苗 候选药物的安全性和免疫原性。背景强调,季节性流感病毒每年都会导致严重疾病和死亡,而目前的疫苗主要针对血凝素,由于病毒变化,其可能并不总是有效。
设计与对象
该研究是一项随机 1/2 期试验,评估基于 mRNA 的四价 季节性流感 疫苗,编码 A/H1N1、A/H3N2、B/Victoria 和 B/Yamagata 的 HA 和 NA。第一阶段共有 293 名 18-50 岁的参与者,第二阶段包括 480 名年轻人(18-64 岁)和 483 名老年人(65-85 岁)。
核心方法
该研究涉及使用 12 种 mRNA 疫苗 制剂中的一种(具有不同的仅 HA 或 HA 和 NA mRNA 剂量)或许可的四价 季节性流感 比较剂。在第 2 阶段,参与者接受具有不同 HA/NA mRNA 剂量的 FLUm4HA-4NA 或适合年龄的许可四价比较剂 疫苗。
主要结果
FLUm4HA-4NA 引起的诱发事件比对照组更常见,但这些副作用大多是轻度或中度且短暂的。没有死亡或 疫苗 相关严重不良事件的报告。
解释与创新
结果表明,与比较物疫苗相比,mRNA 疫苗诱导免疫应答产生HA和NA蛋白,针对漂移的A菌株具有相似或更高的HI和NI效价。研究还发现,FLUm4HA-4NA 后的 HA 特异性 T 细胞反应普遍高于对照组 疫苗接种 后的反应。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持该 mRNA HA/NA 疫苗 的持续开发,特别是针对 B 型流感病毒株改进 免疫应答 的努力。疫苗 可接受的安全性和免疫原性表明有可能增强季节性流感预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于样本量有限的单项试验,由于缺乏长期随访以及不同年龄组的 免疫应答 存在潜在变异性,证据不充分。

A Phase 1/2 Trial Evaluating the Safety and Immunogenicity of an mRNA-based Seasonal Influenza Vaccine Candidate Encoding Hemagglutinin and Neuraminidase.

Authors: Ilse De Coster, Jose F. Cardona, Murdo Ferguson, Isabel Leroux-Roels, Haylene Nell, Stefano Berrè, Sonia Schoonbroodt, Clarisse Lorin, Leroy Jide Ovbude, Brian Moldt
Original abstract (English)
Hemagglutinin (HA) is the principal immunologic target of most licensed seasonal influenza vaccines. Targeting both HA and neuraminidase (NA) might improve vaccine effectiveness. This randomized phase 1/2 study evaluated an investigational mRNA-based quadrivalent seasonal influenza vaccine, encoding HA and NA of A/H1N1, A/H3N2, B/Victoria, and B/Yamagata (FLUm4HA-4NA). In phase 1, adults aged 18–50 years received either one of twelve mRNA vaccine formulations of varying HA-only or HA and NA mRNA doses, or a licensed quadrivalent seasonal influenza comparator. In phase 2, participants aged 18–64 years (younger adults) and 65–85 years (older adults) received FLUm4HA-4NA (three groups per age category, with different HA/NA mRNA doses) or age-appropriate licensed quadrivalent comparator. Primary endpoints were safety/reactogenicity, and hemagglutination inhibition [HI] and NA inhibition [NI] responses against the four seasonal strains at day 29. Cross-reactive HI/NI responses against drifted strains, virus neutralization, and HA-/NA-specific cell-mediated immunity were evaluated in phase 2 as tertiary objectives. A total of 293 participants (21–25 per group) were vaccinated in phase 1, 480 younger (108–137 per group) and 483 older adults (117–125 per group) in phase 2. Solicited events were more frequent with FLUm4HA-4NA than comparators and were mostly short-lived and mild or moderate. No deaths or vaccine-related serious adverse events were reported. Post-vaccination HI and neutralizing titers in the FLUm4HA-4NA groups were similar or higher for the A strains and lower for the B strains versus comparators. NI responses exceeded those of comparators for all four strains. HI and NI titers against evaluated drifted A strains were similar or higher after FLUm4HA-4NA than after comparator vaccination. HA-specific CD4+ and CD8+ T-cell responses were generally higher after FLUm4HA-4NA than comparator vaccination. FLUm4HA-4NA showed an acceptable safety profile and humoral and cellular immunogenicity in the study population, supporting further development and formulation optimization to improve influenza B HI responses. Seasonal flu causes serious illness and death each year. Vaccination can help prevent flu, but currently available flu vaccines are not always fully effective. This is because flu viruses change often, so the strains in the vaccine may not closely match the strains spreading each season. Current flu vaccines mainly target one viral protein called hemagglutinin (HA). Adding another viral protein, neuraminidase (NA), may improve vaccine effectiveness. Our study tested a new mRNA-based seasonal flu vaccine designed to target both HA and NA from the four major circulating flu strains. The study had a phase 1 part, analyzing 293 adults aged 18–50 years, and a phase 2 part, analyzing 480 younger adults (18–64 years) and 483 older adults (65–85 years). In each part, participants received one of several mRNA vaccine formulations (with various HA and NA mRNA doses) or an age-appropriate licensed flu vaccine. More people who received the mRNA vaccine had side effects than those who received the licensed vaccines. These side effects were usually short-lived and were mostly mild or moderate. The mRNA flu vaccine induced an immune response to both the HA and NA target proteins. For HA, the immune response was similar or higher than that of the licensed vaccines for two of the flu strains (A strains), and lower for the other two strains (B strains). For NA, responses were higher for all strains. These results support continued development of this mRNA HA/NA vaccine, including improvement of the response to the flu B strains.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the safety and immunogenicity of an mRNA-based seasonal influenza vaccine candidate that encodes both hemagglutinin and neuraminidase proteins. The background highlights that seasonal influenza viruses cause serious illness and death annually, and current vaccines primarily target hemagglutinin, which may not always be effective due to viral changes.
Design and population
The study was a randomized phase 1/2 trial evaluating an mRNA-based quadrivalent seasonal influenza vaccine, encoding HA and NA of A/H1N1, A/H3N2, B/Victoria, and B/Yamagata. A total of 293 participants aged 18–50 years were included in phase 1, and 480 younger adults (18–64 years) and 483 older adults (65–85 years) were included in phase 2.
Core methods
The study involved administering one of twelve mRNA vaccine formulations with varying HA-only or HA and NA mRNA doses, or a licensed quadrivalent seasonal influenza comparator. In phase 2, participants received FLUm4HA-4NA with different HA/NA mRNA doses or age-appropriate licensed quadrivalent comparator vaccines.
Main results
Solicited events were more frequent with FLUm4HA-4NA than with comparators, but these side effects were mostly mild or moderate and short-lived. No deaths or vaccine-related serious adverse events were reported.
Interpretation and novelty
The results indicate that the mRNA vaccine induced immune responses to both HA and NA proteins, with similar or higher HI and NI titers against drifted A strains compared to comparator vaccines. The study also found that HA-specific T-cell responses were generally higher after FLUm4HA-4NA than after comparator vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the continued development of this mRNA HA/NA vaccine, particularly with efforts to improve immune responses to influenza B strains. The vaccine's acceptable safety profile and immunogenicity suggest potential for enhancing seasonal flu prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on a single trial with a limited sample size, and the evidence is moderate due to the lack of long-term follow-up and potential variability in immune responses across different age groups.

Journal: International journal of infectious diseases : IJID : official publication of the International Society for Infectious Diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-17  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.ijid.2026.109188  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 109188
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2025-2026 年流感季节期间流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 K 谱系的非典型全球传播。

Non-canonical global dissemination of the influenza A(H3N2) K lineage during the 2025-2026 influenza season.
作者： Li-Rong Cao, Yan Li, Hong Zheng, Jing-He Fang, Xiang-Yu Zeng, Shi-Min Su, Jingzhi Lou, Cai-Qi Liu, Zhi-Hui Zhang, Shi Zhao
摘要中文翻译背景:季节性流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 表现出由连续抗原 进化 驱动的快速全球谱系更替。然而,新的主导 H3N2 谱系的出现仍不完全清楚。方法:我们将大规模 基因组监测 与系统发育重建、时间 突变 动力学分析、地理传播分析和结构表征相结合,以研究 H3N2 K 谱系的出现和全球扩张。从 GISAID 检索了 1992 年至 2026 年间全球收集的总共 170,793 个完整的人 H3N2 血凝素 (HA) 序列。结果:我们发现 K 谱系在一个流感季节内迅速取代了之前共同传播的 H3N2 J.2 亚谱系。其全球扩张与以美国为中心的非规范传播模式有关,而不是传统的东亚东南亚(E-SEA)源系统。对全球 监测 数据的时间 突变 动态分析表明,K 谱系定义替换首先在美国和日本被发现。尽管积累了多个抗原位点取代,但结构分析表明总体 HA 结构稳定性得以保留,与预先存在的抗体之间具有差异相容性,而不是完全抗体逃逸。结论:我们的研究结果扩展了目前对全球主导的 H3N2 变异如何出现的理解,并表明地理分布的进化组装可能代表了当代流感 进化 的重要途径。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 2025-2026 年流感季节期间流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 K 谱系在全球的快速传播,解决尚不完全了解的新主导 H3N2 谱系的出现问题。
设计与对象
该研究将大规模 基因组监测 与系统发育重建、时间 突变 动力学分析、地理传播分析和结构表征相结合,以研究 H3N2 K 谱系的出现和全球扩张。
核心方法
该研究采用 基因组监测 和结构表征分析了 1992 年至 2026 年间 GISAID 在全球收集的 170,793 个完整的人类 H3N2 血凝素 (HA) 序列。
主要结果
K 谱系在一个流感季节内迅速取代了之前共同传播的 H3N2 J.2 亚谱系,其全球扩张遵循以美国为中心的非规范模式。时间 突变 动力学分析表明,K 谱系定义取代首先在美国和日本发现,结构分析表明总体 HA 结构稳定性得到保留。
解释与创新
这些发现扩展了目前对全球主导的 H3N2 变异如何出现的理解,表明地理分布的进化组装可能代表了当代流感 进化 的重要途径。
科研与公卫意义
该研究的结果可以为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 的流感策略提供信息,特别是在了解新的 H3N2 谱系的快速出现和全球传播方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究对 基因组监测 数据的依赖以及临床数据的缺乏限制了对研究结果的解释。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Non-canonical global dissemination of the influenza A(H3N2) K lineage during the 2025-2026 influenza season.

Authors: Li-Rong Cao, Yan Li, Hong Zheng, Jing-He Fang, Xiang-Yu Zeng, Shi-Min Su, Jingzhi Lou, Cai-Qi Liu, Zhi-Hui Zhang, Shi Zhao
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Seasonal influenza A(H3N2) exhibits rapid global lineage turnover driven by continuous antigenic evolution. However, the emergence of newly dominant H3N2 lineages remains incompletely understood. METHODS: We combined large-scale genomic surveillance with phylogenetic reconstruction, temporal mutation dynamics analyses, geographic dissemination analyses, and structural characterization to investigate emergence and global expansion of the H3N2 K lineage. A total of 170,793 complete human H3N2 hemagglutinin (HA) sequences collected globally between 1992 and 2026 were retrieved from GISAID. RESULTS: We found that the K lineage rapidly replaced previously co-circulating H3N2 J.2 sublineages within a single influenza season. Its global expansion was associated with a non-canonical dissemination pattern centered on the United States rather than the traditional East-Southeast Asia (E-SEA) source system. Temporal mutation dynamics analyses of global surveillance data showed that K-lineage-defining substitutions were first detected in the United States and Japan. Despite accumulation of multiple antigenic-site substitutions, structural analyses suggested preservation of overall HA structural stability, with differential compatibility across pre-existing antibodies rather than complete antibody escape. CONCLUSIONS: Our findings expand current understanding of how globally dominant H3N2 variants emerge and suggest that geographically distributed evolutionary assembly may represent an important pathway in contemporary influenza evolution.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the rapid global dissemination of the influenza A(H3N2) K lineage during the 2025-2026 influenza season, addressing the incompletely understood emergence of newly dominant H3N2 lineages.
Design and population
The study combined large-scale genomic surveillance with phylogenetic reconstruction, temporal mutation dynamics analyses, geographic dissemination analyses, and structural characterization to investigate the emergence and global expansion of the H3N2 K lineage.
Core methods
The research involved analyzing 170,793 complete human H3N2 hemagglutinin (HA) sequences collected globally between 1992 and 2026 from GISAID, employing genomic surveillance and structural characterization.
Main results
The K lineage rapidly replaced previously co-circulating H3N2 J.2 sublineages within a single influenza season, and its global expansion followed a non-canonical pattern centered on the United States. Temporal mutation dynamics analyses showed that K-lineage-defining substitutions were first detected in the United States and Japan, with structural analyses indicating preservation of overall HA structural stability.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings expand current understanding of how globally dominant H3N2 variants emerge, suggesting that geographically distributed evolutionary assembly may represent an important pathway in contemporary influenza evolution.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study’s findings could inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for influenza, particularly in understanding the rapid emergence and global spread of new H3N2 lineages.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s reliance on genomic surveillance data and the absence of clinical data limit the interpretation of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

黎巴嫩医院医护人员中的 季节性流感 疫苗接种:COVID-19 大流行和经济危机后的决定因素

Seasonal influenza vaccination among healthcare workers in Lebanese hospitals: determinants of uptake post-COVID-19 pandemic and economic crisis
作者： Nour Ibrahim, Aysar Fakih, Rana Attieh, Shadi Eid, Jame Tannous, Mariana Boulos, Lyna Abo Merhi, Ibrahim Ismail, Mona Youssef, Hassan Mallat
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 2023 年至 2024 年黎巴嫩医院医护人员 (HCW) 自我报告的流感 疫苗接种 感染情况,与大流行前的 2018-2019 年感染情况进行比较,并确定与非 疫苗接种 相关的因素。医护人员感染和传播流感的风险增加。
设计与对象
2024 年 4 月至 7 月,对黎巴嫩多个地区 14 家公立和私立医院的医护人员进行了一项横断面调查。
核心方法
该调查评估了流感 疫苗接种 状况、免费机构 疫苗 提供、意识、态度以及与 COVID-19 疫苗 相关的看法。多变量逻辑回归确定了 2023-2024 年与非 疫苗接种 独立相关的因素。
主要结果
在 391 名参与者中,2023-2024 年自我报告的流感 疫苗接种 摄入量低于 2018-2019 年(29.9% vs 60.5%,p < 0.0001)。免费机构 疫苗 拨款也较低(39.6% vs 70.1%,p < 0.0001),19.2% 愿意自付费用。
解释与创新
在年龄 <30 岁的医护人员、非医生、没有免费机构 疫苗 服务的人员、对流感 疫苗接种 态度不利的人员以及对 COVID-19 疫苗 相关犹豫中度至高度的人员中,观察到非 疫苗接种 的几率较高。
科研与公卫意义
恢复基于工作场所的免费 疫苗接种 并解决与 COVID-19 疫苗 相关的犹豫可能会提高资源有限的医疗保健环境中的使用率。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系,并且可能存在自我报告偏差。样本量和区域多样性可能会影响普遍性。

Seasonal influenza vaccination among healthcare workers in Lebanese hospitals: determinants of uptake post-COVID-19 pandemic and economic crisis

Authors: Nour Ibrahim, Aysar Fakih, Rana Attieh, Shadi Eid, Jame Tannous, Mariana Boulos, Lyna Abo Merhi, Ibrahim Ismail, Mona Youssef, Hassan Mallat
Original abstract (English)
Background Healthcare workers (HCWs) are at increased risk of acquiring and transmitting influenza. We assessed self-reported influenza vaccination uptake among surveyed HCWs in Lebanese hospitals during 2023–2024, compared it with their reported uptake in pre-pandemic 2018–2019, and identified factors associated with non-vaccination. Methods This cross-sectional survey was conducted from April to July 2024 among HCWs from 14 public and private hospitals across multiple regions of Lebanon. The questionnaire assessed influenza vaccination status, free institutional vaccine provision, awareness, attitudes, and COVID-19 vaccine–related perceptions. Multivariable logistic regression identified factors independently associated with non-vaccination in 2023–2024. Results Among 391 participants, self-reported influenza vaccination uptake was lower in 2023–2024 than in 2018–2019 (29.9% vs 60.5%, p < 0.0001). Free institutional vaccine provision was also lower (39.6% vs 70.1%, p < 0.0001), and 19.2% were willing to pay out of pocket. Higher odds of non-vaccination were observed among HCWs aged <30 years (aOR 3.80, 95% CI 1.96–7.68), non-physicians (aOR 5.17, 95% CI 2.26–12.42), those without free institutional vaccine provision (aOR 17.74, 95% CI 9.38–35.72), those with unfavorable influenza vaccination attitudes (aOR 4.61, 95% CI 2.10–10.63), and those with moderate-to-high COVID-19 vaccine–related hesitancy (aOR 2.09, 95% CI 1.03–4.27). Conclusion Among surveyed HCWs, influenza vaccination uptake in 2023–2024 was substantially lower than reported for 2018–2019. Lack of free institutional vaccine provision and unfavorable vaccine-related perceptions were independently associated with non-vaccination. Restoring free workplace-based vaccination and addressing COVID-19 vaccine–related hesitancy may improve uptake in resource-constrained healthcare settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess self-reported influenza vaccination uptake among healthcare workers (HCWs) in Lebanese hospitals during 2023–2024, comparing it with pre-pandemic 2018–2019 uptake, and identified factors associated with non-vaccination. HCWs are at increased risk of acquiring and transmitting influenza.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted from April to July 2024 among HCWs from 14 public and private hospitals across multiple regions of Lebanon.
Core methods
The survey assessed influenza vaccination status, free institutional vaccine provision, awareness, attitudes, and COVID-19 vaccine-related perceptions. Multivariable logistic regression identified factors independently associated with non-vaccination in 2023–2024.
Main results
Among 391 participants, self-reported influenza vaccination uptake was lower in 2023–2024 than in 2018–2019 (29.9% vs 60.5%, p < 0.0001). Free institutional vaccine provision was also lower (39.6% vs 70.1%, p < 0.0001), and 19.2% were willing to pay out of pocket.
Interpretation and novelty
Higher odds of non-vaccination were observed among HCWs aged <30 years, non-physicians, those without free institutional vaccine provision, those with unfavorable influenza vaccination attitudes, and those with moderate-to-high COVID-19 vaccine-related hesitancy.
Scientific and public-health significance
Restoring free workplace-based vaccination and addressing COVID-19 vaccine-related hesitancy may improve uptake in resource-constrained healthcare settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and the potential for self-report bias. The sample size and regional diversity may affect generalizability.

2026 年布隆迪一所中学流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 K 分支 暴发 的基因组调查

Genomic investigation of an Influenza A(H3N2) subclade K outbreak in a secondary school in Burundi, 2026
作者： Néhémie Nzoyikorera, Leonard Schuele, David F. Nieuwenhuijse, Cassien Nduwimana, Armstrong Ndihokubwayo, Théogène Ihorimbere, Denis Niyomwungere, Hayley Cassidy, Marjan Boter, Alexis Niyomwungere
摘要中文翻译严重急性呼吸道感染(SARI)是一个重要的公共卫生问题,特别是在识别致病病原体具有挑战性的环境中。对患有不明原因 SARI (SARI 监测) 的患者进行检测和确定致病病原体的特征也被视为新出现疾病防范的重要支柱。因此,我们应用靶向病毒富集测序作为了解广泛疾病病因的工具。2026 年 1 月,布隆迪布琼布拉一所中学的呼吸道疾病病例有所增加,引发了人们对 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 可能卷土重来的担忧。然而,对有症状个体的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2) 检测呈阴性,表明另一种呼吸道病原体参与其中。为了确定病原体,使用靶向病毒富集测序对从有症状的学生收集的鼻咽样本进行了研究。使用 iSeq 100 平台上的 Illumina Viral 监测 Panel v2 对 8 个 SARS-CoV-2 阴性样本进行了测序。对测序读数进行质量检查和分析以进行病毒鉴定,然后对已鉴定的病毒进行 基因组 重建、进化枝分配和系统发育分析。在八个分析样本中的七个中检测到甲型流感病毒 (IAV)。四个样本产生了接近完整的 IAV 基因组,从而可以进行详细的基因组表征。所有表征的 基因组 均被鉴定为流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 亚支 K,代表布隆迪对该亚支的首次基因组检测和表征。系统发育分析表明,布隆迪序列聚集成两个不同的组,表明在 暴发 期间至少存在两个病毒谱系的循环。未检测到神经氨酸酶抑制剂耐药性相关标记。通过 GISAID 共享流感序列以支持全球流感 监测。这项研究表明,在病因不明的情况下,靶向病毒富集测序对于研究急性呼吸道疾病 暴发 非常有用,特别是在初始检测结果呈阴性且其他呼吸道病毒的常规检测有限的情况下。我们首次提供了布隆迪流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 K 亚支的基因组特征。不同病毒谱系的鉴定凸显了基因组方法在补充常规实验室检测和支持布隆迪 暴发 调查和呼吸道病原体 监测 方面的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
严重急性呼吸道感染(SARI)是一个重要的公共卫生问题,特别是在识别致病病原体具有挑战性的环境中。对患有不明原因 SARI 的患者进行检测并确定致病病原体的特征也被视为新出现疾病防范的重要支柱。
设计与对象
2026 年 1 月,布隆迪布琼布拉一所中学的呼吸道疾病病例有所增加,引发了人们对 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 可能卷土重来的担忧。然而,对有症状个体的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2) 检测呈阴性,表明另一种呼吸道病原体参与其中。
核心方法
为了确定病原体,使用靶向病毒富集测序对从有症状的学生收集的鼻咽样本进行了研究。使用 iSeq 100 平台上的 Illumina Viral 监测 Panel v2 对 8 个 SARS-CoV-2 阴性样本进行了测序。对测序读数进行质量检查和分析以进行病毒鉴定,然后对已鉴定的病毒进行 基因组 重建、进化枝分配和系统发育分析。
主要结果
在八个分析样本中的七个中检测到甲型流感病毒 (IAV)。四个样本产生了接近完整的 IAV 基因组,从而可以进行详细的基因组表征。所有表征的 基因组 均被鉴定为流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 亚支 K,代表布隆迪对该亚支的首次基因组检测和表征。系统发育分析表明,布隆迪序列聚集成两个不同的组,表明在 暴发 期间至少存在两个病毒谱系的循环。
解释与创新
不同病毒谱系的鉴定凸显了基因组方法在补充常规实验室检测和支持布隆迪 暴发 调查和呼吸道病原体 监测 方面的价值。这项研究首次提供了布隆迪流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 K 亚支的基因组特征。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,基因组监测 需要更好地了解布隆迪流感的 流行病学 并支持 暴发 应对策略。他们还强调了当初始测试不确定时,靶向病毒富集测序在识别病原体方面的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量较小和单一 暴发 设置的限制。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于对特定地点和时间段的有限数量样本的基因组分析。

Genomic investigation of an Influenza A(H3N2) subclade K outbreak in a secondary school in Burundi, 2026

Authors: Néhémie Nzoyikorera, Leonard Schuele, David F. Nieuwenhuijse, Cassien Nduwimana, Armstrong Ndihokubwayo, Théogène Ihorimbere, Denis Niyomwungere, Hayley Cassidy, Marjan Boter, Alexis Niyomwungere
Original abstract (English)
Severe acute respiratory infections (SARI) are an important public health concern, particularly in settings where identification of the causative pathogen can be challenging. Testing of patients with unexplained SARI (SARI surveillance) and characterization of causative pathogens also is seen as an important pillar of emerging disease preparedness. Therefore, we applied targeted viral enrichment sequencing as a tool for understanding a broad range of disease etiologies. In January 2026, an increase in respiratory illness cases was reported in a secondary school in Bujumbura, Burundi, raising concerns about a possible resurgence of coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19). However, severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) testing of symptomatic individuals was negative, suggesting the involvement of another respiratory pathogen. To identify the causative agent, nasopharyngeal samples collected from symptomatic students were investigated using targeted viral enrichment sequencing. Eight SARS-CoV-2-negative samples were sequenced using the Illumina Viral Surveillance Panel v2 on the iSeq 100 platform. Sequencing reads were quality-checked and analysed for viral identification, followed by genome reconstruction, clade assignment, and phylogenetic analysis of the identified viruses. Influenza A virus (IAV) was detected in seven of the eight analyzed samples. Four samples yielded near-complete IAV genomes, allowing detailed genomic characterization. All characterized genomes were identified as Influenza A(H3N2) subclade K, representing the first genomic detection and characterization of this subclade in Burundi. Phylogenetic analysis showed that Burundian sequences clustered into two distinct groups, suggesting the circulation of at least two viral lineages during the outbreak. No neuraminidase inhibitor resistance-associated markers were detected. Influenza sequences were shared through GISAID to support global influenza surveillance. This study shows the usefulness of targeted viral-enrichment sequencing for investigating acute respiratory outbreaks when the cause is not known, particularly when initial testing is negative and routine testing for other respiratory viruses is limited. We provide the first genomic characterization of Influenza A(H3N2) subclade K in Burundi. The identification of different viral lineages highlights the value of genomic approaches to complement routine laboratory testing and support outbreak investigation and respiratory pathogen surveillance in Burundi.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Severe acute respiratory infections (SARI) are an important public health concern, particularly in settings where identification of the causative pathogen can be challenging. Testing of patients with unexplained SARI and characterization of causative pathogens also is seen as an important pillar of emerging disease preparedness.
Design and population
In January 2026, an increase in respiratory illness cases was reported in a secondary school in Bujumbura, Burundi, raising concerns about a possible resurgence of coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19). However, severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) testing of symptomatic individuals was negative, suggesting the involvement of another respiratory pathogen.
Core methods
To identify the causative agent, nasopharyngeal samples collected from symptomatic students were investigated using targeted viral enrichment sequencing. Eight SARS-CoV-2-negative samples were sequenced using the Illumina Viral Surveillance Panel v2 on the iSeq 100 platform. Sequencing reads were quality-checked and analysed for viral identification, followed by genome reconstruction, clade assignment, and phylogenetic analysis of the identified viruses.
Main results
Influenza A virus (IAV) was detected in seven of the eight analyzed samples. Four samples yielded near-complete IAV genomes, allowing detailed genomic characterization. All characterized genomes were identified as Influenza A(H3N2) subclade K, representing the first genomic detection and characterization of this subclade in Burundi. Phylogenetic analysis showed that Burundian sequences clustered into two distinct groups, suggesting the circulation of at least two viral lineages during the outbreak.
Interpretation and novelty
The identification of different viral lineages highlights the value of genomic approaches to complement routine laboratory testing and support outbreak investigation and respiratory pathogen surveillance in Burundi. This study provides the first genomic characterization of Influenza A(H3N2) subclade K in Burundi.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for genomic surveillance to better understand the epidemiology of influenza in Burundi and support outbreak response strategies. They also emphasize the importance of targeted viral enrichment sequencing in identifying causative agents when initial tests are inconclusive.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the small sample size and the focus on a single outbreak setting. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on genomic analysis of a limited number of samples from a specific location and time period.

针对废水中甲型流感病毒开发一种健康 基因组监测 工具并对其进行基准测试

Developing and benchmarking one health genomic surveillance tools for influenza A virus in wastewater
作者： Minxi Jiang, Audrey L. W. Wang, James B. Thissen, Kara L. Nelson, Lenore Pipes, Rose S. Kantor
摘要中文翻译摘要:甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 仍然是一种持续存在的健康威胁,废水中的全 基因组 测序提供了一种有前途的 监测 工具。然而,IAV 在废水中丰度较低,因此难以测序。我们对适合多亚型全 基因组 IAV 测序的四种靶向富集方法进行了基准测试,包括定制方法和现成方法。我们的定制 HA 平铺扩增子面板灵敏、快速且经济高效,使其适合监测已知亚型的低丰度季节性变异,尽管它对保守且完整的引物结合位点的依赖限制了引物设计到较少的亚型。已发布的通用扩增子方法针对所有 IAV 亚型,但由于其依赖于完整的 基因组 片段,因此在废水中表现不佳。同时,探针捕获方法对 RNA 降解具有弹性。在真实废水样品中,我们的定制探针板可从循环的 H1N1、H3N2、H5N1 中恢复全 基因组 信号,包括在 dPCR 检测受限的样品中经过验证的 H5N1 2.3.4.4b 读数。然而,探针捕获通常比扩增子方法成本更高且劳动力密集。在所有四种测序方法、两种废水基质以及大多数掺入和内源性病毒靶标中,基于超滤的病毒浓缩始终优于大容量直接提取。这些基准比较和定制面板共同提供了在常规废水 监测 中实施 IAV 基因组测序所需的信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 仍然是一种持续存在的 One Health 威胁,废水中的全 基因组 测序提供了一种有前景的 监测 工具。
设计与对象
我们对适合多亚型全 基因组 IAV 测序的四种靶向富集方法进行了基准测试,包括定制方法和现成方法。
核心方法
在所有四种测序方法、两种废水基质以及大多数掺入和内源性病毒靶标中,基于超滤的病毒浓缩始终优于大容量直接提取。
主要结果
在真实废水样品中,我们的定制探针板可从循环的 H1N1、H3N2、H5N1 中恢复全 基因组 信号,包括在 dPCR 检测受限的样品中经过验证的 H5N1 2.3.4.4b 读数。
解释与创新
我们的定制 HA 平铺扩增子面板灵敏、快速且经济高效,使其适合监测已知亚型的低丰度季节性变异,尽管它对保守且完整的引物结合位点的依赖限制了引物设计到较少的亚型。
科研与公卫意义
这些基准比较和定制面板共同提供了在常规废水 监测 中实施 IAV 基因组测序所需的信息。
局限与证据强度
探针捕获方法通常比扩增子方法更昂贵且劳动密集。

Developing and benchmarking one health genomic surveillance tools for influenza A virus in wastewater

Authors: Minxi Jiang, Audrey L. W. Wang, James B. Thissen, Kara L. Nelson, Lenore Pipes, Rose S. Kantor
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Influenza A viruses (IAV) remain a persistent One Health threat, and whole-genome sequencing from wastewater offers a promising surveillance tool. However, IAV is at low abundance in wastewater, making it difficult to sequence. We benchmark four targeted enrichment methods suited for multi-subtype whole-genome IAV sequencing including custom and off-the-shelf methods. Our custom HA tiled-amplicon panel is sensitive, fast, and cost-effective, making it suitable for monitoring low-abundance seasonal variants of known subtypes, although its reliance on conserved and intact primer-binding sites limits primer design to fewer subtypes. A published universal amplicon method targets all IAV subtypes but performs poorly in wastewater due to its reliance on intact genome segments. Meanwhile, probe-capture methods are resilient to RNA degradation. In real wastewater samples, our custom probe panel recovers whole-genome signals from circulating H1N1, H3N2, H5N1, including validated H5N1 2.3.4.4b reads in samples where dPCR detection is limited. However, probe-capture is generally more costly and labor-intensive than amplicon methods. Across all four sequencing methods, two wastewater matrices, and most spike-in and endogenous virus targets, ultrafiltration-based virus concentration consistently outperforms large-volume direct extraction. Together, these benchmarking comparisons and custom panels provide needed information for implementing IAV genomic sequencing in routine wastewater surveillance.

Main results

出生 队列研究 对与流感相关的成人的影响 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 疫苗 有效性。

Birth Cohort Effects in Adults Associated With Influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 Vaccine Effectiveness.
作者： Feng Liu, F Liaini Gross, Zhunan Li, Stacey L House, Tamair Curley, Elie A Saade, Zainab Albar, Richard K Zimmerman, Mary Patricia Nowalk, Manjusha Gaglani
摘要中文翻译背景:在2023-2024年流感季节,1957-1985年出生的队列研究的成年人对疫苗病毒的疫苗病毒的有效性(VE)低于其他年龄组,包括1986-2005年出生的队列研究的成年人。我们研究了从流感病例和对照中收集的血清中的 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 抗体水平,以解释低 VE 对出生 队列研究 的影响。方法:从 2023 年至 2024 年期间收集成人(1986 年至 2005 年和 1957 年至 1985 年出生的 队列研究)的急性和恢复期血清。针对代表性 A(H1N1) 病毒和血凝素抗原评估了血凝抑制 (HI) 和结合抗体。结果:1957-1985 年出生的 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 感染病例的急性血清中针对循环 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒的 HI 抗体明显低于 1986-2005 年出生的 队列研究。1957-1985 年出生的 队列研究 病例中,超过 60% 的流行 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒的 HI 抗体≤10。尽管如此,在同一 1957-1985 年出生的 队列研究 中接种疫苗的病例对 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒感染产生了强烈的抗体反应,表明 疫苗 免疫原性较低。抗体格局分析显示,这些 1957-1985 年出生的 VE 最低的患者可能感染了类似 USSR/77 的 A(H1N1) 病毒。结论:急性血清中的 HI 抗体水平与预防流感 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 感染相关。提高 疫苗 的免疫原性有助于提高 VE 并克服出生 队列研究 效应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在2023-2024年流感季节,与其他年龄组(包括1986-2005年出生的队列研究的成年人)相比,1957-1985年出生的队列研究的成年人对甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒的疫苗病毒的有效性(VE)最低。该研究旨在调查从流感病例和对照中收集的血清中的 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 抗体水平,以解释低 VE 对出生 队列研究 的影响。
设计与对象
该研究涉及在 2023 年至 2024 年期间收集成人(1986 年至 2005 年和 1957 年至 1985 年出生的 队列研究)的急性和恢复期血清。针对代表性 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒和血凝素抗原评估了血凝抑制 (HI) 和结合抗体。
核心方法
2023-2024 年期间从成人(1986-2005 年和 1957-1985 年出生的 队列研究)收集急性和恢复期血清。
主要结果
1957-1985 年出生的 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 感染病例的急性血清中针对循环 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒的 HI 抗体明显低于 1986-2005 年出生的 队列研究。1957-1985 年出生的 队列研究 病例中,超过 60% 的流行 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒的 HI 抗体≤10。抗体格局分析显示,这些 1957-1985 年出生的 VE 最低的患者可能感染了类似 USSR/77 的 A(H1N1) 病毒。
解释与创新
急性血清中的 HI 抗体水平与预防流感 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 感染有关。该研究表明,1957-1985 年出生的 疫苗 免疫原性较低,这可以解释观察到的低 VE 出生 队列研究 效应。
科研与公卫意义
提高 疫苗 的免疫原性有助于提高 VE 并克服出生 队列研究 效应。研究结果对 疫苗 的发展和加强老年人流感保护的公共卫生战略具有影响。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性包括研究的观察性质和潜在的混杂因素。尽管如此,1957-1985 年出生的 队列研究 的疫苗接种病例中强烈的抗体反应表明 疫苗 的免疫原性较低,这是一个重要的发现。证据强度中等。

Birth Cohort Effects in Adults Associated With Influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 Vaccine Effectiveness.

Authors: Feng Liu, F Liaini Gross, Zhunan Li, Stacey L House, Tamair Curley, Elie A Saade, Zainab Albar, Richard K Zimmerman, Mary Patricia Nowalk, Manjusha Gaglani
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: In 2023-2024 influenza season, adults in the 1957-1985 birth cohort had the lowest vaccine effectiveness (VE) against A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses than other age groups, including those in the 1986-2005 birth cohort. We investigated A(H1N1)pdm09 antibody levels in sera collected from influenza cases and controls to explain the birth cohort effect of the low VE. METHODS: Acute and convalescent sera were collected from adults (1986-2005 and 1957-1985 birth cohorts) during 2023-2024. Hemagglutination inhibiting (HI) and binding antibodies were assessed against representative A(H1N1) viruses and hemagglutinin antigens. RESULTS: A(H1N1)pdm09-infected cases in the 1957-1985 birth cohort had significantly lower HI antibodies in the acute sera against circulating A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses than 1986-2005 birth cohort. Over 60% of the cases in the 1957-1985 birth cohort had HI antibodies ≤10 to circulating A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses. Nonetheless, vaccinated cases in the same 1957-1985 birth cohort mounted robust antibody responses to A(H1N1)pdm09 virus infection, suggesting low vaccine immunogenicity. Antibody landscape analysis revealed that these patients born in 1957-1985 with the lowest VE were likely primed with USSR/77-like A(H1N1) viruses. CONCLUSIONS: HI antibody levels in the acute sera were associated with protection against influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 infection. Improving the immunogenicity of the vaccines could help improve VE and overcome the birth cohort effects.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In 2023-2024 influenza season, adults in the 1957-1985 birth cohort had the lowest vaccine effectiveness (VE) against A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses compared to other age groups, including those in the 1986-2005 birth cohort. The study aimed to investigate A(H1N1)pdm09 antibody levels in sera collected from influenza cases and controls to explain the birth cohort effect of the low VE.
Design and population
The study involved collecting acute and convalescent sera from adults (1986-2005 and 1957-1985 birth cohorts) during 2023-2024. Hemagglutination inhibiting (HI) and binding antibodies were assessed against representative A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses and hemagglutinin antigens.
Core methods
Acute and convalescent sera were collected from adults (1986-2005 and 1957-1985 birth cohorts) during 2023-2024.
Main results
A(H1N1)pdm09-infected cases in the 1957-1985 birth cohort had significantly lower HI antibodies in the acute sera against circulating A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses than 1986-2005 birth cohort. Over 60% of the cases in the 1957-1985 birth cohort had HI antibodies ≤10 to circulating A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses. Antibody landscape analysis revealed that these patients born in 1957-1985 with the lowest VE were likely primed with USSR/77-like A(H1N1) viruses.
Interpretation and novelty
HI antibody levels in the acute sera were associated with protection against influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 infection. The study suggests low vaccine immunogenicity in the 1957-1985 birth cohort, which could explain the observed birth cohort effect of low VE.
Scientific and public-health significance
Improving the immunogenicity of the vaccines could help improve VE and overcome the birth cohort effects. The findings have implications for vaccine development and public health strategies to enhance influenza protection in older adults.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations include the observational nature of the study and the potential for confounding factors. Nonetheless, the robust antibody responses in vaccinated cases in the 1957-1985 birth cohort suggest low vaccine immunogenicity, which is a significant finding. The evidence strength is moderate.

由计算抗体相似性测量驱动的 H3N2 甲型流感病毒 HA 靶向抗体表位的快速鉴定

Rapid Identification of H3N2 Influenza A Virus HA-Targeting Antibody Epitopes Driven by Computational Antibody Similarity Measures
作者： Zheng Zhu, Zheng-Shan Chen, Jin-Xia Wu, Jin Han, Lin-Kuo Li, Ke-Wen Qian, Yue Cui, Ze-Ya Li, Rong-Jun Ding, Shi-Xin Pang
摘要中文翻译摘要 抗体是预防和预防流感的重要治疗药物,而血凝素(HA)是开发治疗性抗体的主要靶点。然而,在此类治疗性抗体的开发中,表位的鉴定仍然是一个未满足的需求。本研究通过对所有抗体-HA 复合物进行计算分析,研究抗体-HA 结合界面(即表位和互补位)的特征,这些复合物具有来自 SAbDab(迄今为止报道的最大的抗体结构数据集)的非冗余实验确定的结构。经过实验鉴定和选择H3N2 HA特异性结合抗体后,建立了参考抗体库。对于结构未知的抗 H3N2 HA 抗体,基于竞争性结合测定进行表位分组,该测定评估对参考文库中抗体结合的干扰。基于表位和互补位结构之间的互补关系,我们选择了与表位收敛最相关的基于结构的相似性度量。此外,我们优先考虑候选抗体与其结构最相似的参考抗体之间的结合干扰的实验验证。我们提出了一个集成的计算和实验框架。该方法包括构建 H3N2 特异性参考抗体集、抗体计算模型、抗体相似性测量,并辅以抗体竞争结合测定。这里提出的干湿迭代方案对先前未表征的抗体 P2A10 的中和效力和广度进行了准确评估。总之,所提出的流程提供了一种强大的方法,用于识别抗 H3N2 HA 抗体上的表位和发现表位靶向抗体,同时证明结构分析在减少实验劳动力方面的有效性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决鉴定针对 H3N2 HA 的治疗性抗体表位方面尚未满足的需求,这对于流感的预防和 治疗 非常重要。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对 SAbDab 数据库中的所有抗体-HA 复合物进行计算分析,以确定 H3N2 HA 特异性结合抗体的表位。
核心方法
这些方法包括 H3N2 特异性参考抗体组的构建、抗体的计算模型、抗体相似性的测量以及抗体竞争结合测定。
主要结果
该研究通过计算和实验方法鉴定并验证了 H3N2 HA 特异性结合抗体的表位,包括优先考虑候选抗体与其结构最相似的参考抗体之间的结合干扰的实验验证。
解释与创新
该研究提出了一个集成的计算和实验框架,用于识别抗 H3N2 HA 抗体上的表位,证明了结构分析在减少实验劳动方面的有效性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为识别抗 H3N2 HA 抗体上的表位和发现表位靶向抗体提供了一种强大的方法,这可以为流感治疗抗体的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括对计算方法的依赖和需要实验验证,这可能会限制研究结果的普遍性。

Rapid Identification of H3N2 Influenza A Virus HA-Targeting Antibody Epitopes Driven by Computational Antibody Similarity Measures

Authors: Zheng Zhu, Zheng-Shan Chen, Jin-Xia Wu, Jin Han, Lin-Kuo Li, Ke-Wen Qian, Yue Cui, Ze-Ya Li, Rong-Jun Ding, Shi-Xin Pang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Antibodies are important therapeutic agents for the prevention and treatment of influenza, and hemagglutinin (HA) serves as the primary target for the development of therapeutic antibodies. However, the identification of epitopes remains an unmet need in the development of such therapeutic antibodies. This study investigates the characteristics of antibody–HA binding interfaces (i.e., epitopes and paratopes) through a computational analysis of all antibody–HA complexes with nonredundant experimentally determined structures from SAbDab, the largest reported antibody structure data set to date. After experimentally identifying and selecting H3N2 HA-specific binding antibodies, a reference antibody library was established. For anti-H3N2 HA antibodies with unknown structures, epitope grouping was conducted based on competitive binding assays that assess interference with the binding of antibodies from the reference library. Based on the complementary relationship between epitope and paratope structures, we selected the structure-based similarity measure most relevant to epitope convergence. Furthermore, we prioritized the experimental validation of binding interference between candidate antibodies and their most structurally similar reference antibodies. We present an integrated computational and experimental framework. This approach includes the construction of an H3N2-specific reference antibody set, computational modeling of antibodies, measurement of antibody similarity, and is complemented by antibody competition binding assays. The dry–wet iterative protocol presented here yielded accurate assessment of the neutralizing potency and breadth of the previously uncharacterized antibody P2A10. Together, the presented pipeline offers a powerful approach for identifying epitopes on anti-H3N2 HA antibodies and for epitope-targeted antibody discovery, while demonstrating the effectiveness of structural analytics in reducing experimental labor.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the unmet need in identifying epitopes for therapeutic antibodies targeting H3N2 HA, which are important for the prevention and treatment of influenza.
Design and population
The study involved computational analysis of all antibody–HA complexes from the SAbDab database to identify epitopes for H3N2 HA-specific binding antibodies.
Core methods
The methods included the construction of an H3N2-specific reference antibody set, computational modeling of antibodies, measurement of antibody similarity, and antibody competition binding assays.
Main results
The study identified and validated epitopes for H3N2 HA-specific binding antibodies through computational and experimental approaches, including the prioritization of experimental validation of binding interference between candidate antibodies and their most structurally similar reference antibodies.
Interpretation and novelty
The study presented an integrated computational and experimental framework for identifying epitopes on anti-H3N2 HA antibodies, demonstrating the effectiveness of structural analytics in reducing experimental labor.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a powerful approach for identifying epitopes on anti-H3N2 HA antibodies and for epitope-targeted antibody discovery, which can inform the development of therapeutic antibodies for influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the reliance on computational methods and the need for experimental validation, which may limit the generalizability of the findings.

Journal: Frontiers in Immunology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-14  |  DOI: 10.3389/fimmu.2026.1944713  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 17
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

T细胞Cyp27b1促进H1N1甲型流感病毒感染小鼠模型中颗粒酶B表达和抗病毒药物 宿主防御

T cell Cyp27b1 promotes granzyme B expression and antiviral host defense in a mouse model of H1N1 influenza A virus infection
作者： Nicole E. Froelich, Mengzhu Tang, Nicholas J. Hanna, Troy C. Sutton, Mark B. Meyer, Margherita T. Cantorna
摘要中文翻译简介 免疫细胞表达维生素 D 受体 (VDR) 和产生 VDR 配体 1,25(OH) 2 D 所需的 CYP27B1 酶;表明维生素 D 反应可能在感染部位受到局部调节。在这里,我们研究了免疫细胞衍生的 1,25(OH) 2 D 在 宿主 抵抗流感感染中的作用。方法用小鼠适应的H1N1甲型流感病毒感染小鼠,测定CYP27B1对宿主抵抗流感感染的作用。感染的小鼠品系包括:野生型 (WT) 同窝小鼠、全身 Cyp27b1 敲除 (Cyp27b1 KO) 小鼠、缺乏肾脏 1,25(OH) 2 D 产生的小鼠(双内含子 (DI)KO)、Cyp27b1 T 细胞特异性缺失小鼠 (T-Cyp27b1 KO) 和 Vdr T 细胞特异性缺失小鼠 (T-Vdr KO)。结果与 WT 同窝小鼠相比,全身 Cyp27b1 KO 小鼠出现严重的甲型流感病毒疾病,包括感染诱导的 死亡率。给Cyp27b1 KO小鼠口服治疗 1,25(OH) 2 D未能完全保护Cyp27b1 KO小鼠免受甲型流感病毒感染。没有肾脏 1,25(OH) 2 D 的小鼠与 WT 同窝小鼠具有相似的流感疾病。与 WT 同窝小鼠相比,T 细胞特异性 Cyp27b1 KO 的流感感染导致呼吸窘迫和嗜睡增加。与全身 Cyp27b1 KO 或 T-Cyp27b1 KO 小鼠相比,WT 和 DIKO 小鼠具有更多的 CD8+ T 细胞,表达更高量的颗粒酶 B+ (GrzB) 和 PD-1 破坏。讨论 该工作表明,宿主 对这种甲型流感病毒感染的抵抗力不需要肾脏 1,25(OH) 2 D。这项工作进一步证明,免疫细胞,特别是 T 细胞可以产生 1,25(OH) 2 D,以局部支持 CD8+ T 细胞对甲型流感病毒感染的反应。需要定义调节 T 细胞或其他免疫细胞类型局部 1,25(OH) 2 D 产生的机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究免疫细胞衍生的 1,25(OH) 2 D 在 宿主 抵抗流感感染中的作用,其背景是免疫细胞表达维生素 D 受体和 CYP27B1 酶,这些酶参与产生 VDR 配体 1,25(OH) 2 D。
设计与对象
该研究使用了甲型H1N1流感病毒感染的小鼠模型,检查了各种小鼠品系,包括野生型(WT)同窝小鼠、全身Cyp27b1敲除(Cyp27b1 KO)小鼠、缺乏肾脏1,25(OH)2 D产生的小鼠(双内含子(DI)KO)、T细胞特异性删除Cyp27b1的小鼠(T-Cyp27b1 KO)和T细胞特异性删除的小鼠Vdr (T-Vdr KO)。
核心方法
小鼠感染适应小鼠的 H1N1 甲型流感病毒,以确定 CYP27B1 在 宿主 耐药性中的作用,特别注意 Cyp27b1 缺失对不同小鼠品系的影响。
主要结果
与 WT 同窝小鼠相比,全身 Cyp27b1 KO 小鼠出现严重的甲型流感病毒疾病,包括感染诱导的 死亡率。与 WT 同窝小鼠相比,T 细胞特异性 Cyp27b1 KO 小鼠表现出呼吸窘迫和嗜睡增加。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,免疫细胞,特别是 T 细胞,可能会局部产生 1,25(OH) 2 D,以支持 CD8+ T 细胞对甲型流感病毒感染的反应,突显 CYP27B1 在 抗病毒药物 宿主 防御中的新作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为了解流感感染期间免疫细胞中 1,25(OH) 2 D 产生的局部调节提供了见解,这可以为未来免疫调节和潜在治疗策略的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠模型,证据不充分,因为它没有完全解决 1,25(OH) 2 D 在人类流感感染中的作用。

T cell Cyp27b1 promotes granzyme B expression and antiviral host defense in a mouse model of H1N1 influenza A virus infection

Authors: Nicole E. Froelich, Mengzhu Tang, Nicholas J. Hanna, Troy C. Sutton, Mark B. Meyer, Margherita T. Cantorna
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Immune cells express vitamin D receptors (VDR) and the CYP27B1 enzyme needed to produce the VDR ligand, 1,25(OH) 2 D; suggesting that perhaps the vitamin D response is regulated locally at the site of infection. Here, we examined the role of immune cell-derived 1,25(OH) 2 D in host resistance to influenza infection. Methods Mice were infected with mouse-adapted H1N1 influenza A virus, to determine the role of CYP27B1 on host resistance to influenza infection. The mouse strains infected include; wild type (WT) littermates, whole-body Cyp27b1 knockout (Cyp27b1 KO) mice, mice lacking renal 1,25(OH) 2 D production (double intronic (DI)KO), mice with T cell-specific deletion of Cyp27b1 (T-Cyp27b1 KO), and mice with T cell-specific deletion of Vdr (T-Vdr KO). Results Whole body Cyp27b1 KO mice developed severe influenza A virus disease, including infection induced mortality, compared to WT littermates. Oral treatments of the Cyp27b1 KO mice with 1,25(OH) 2 D failed to completely protect the Cyp27b1 KO mice from influenza A virus disease. Mice without renal 1,25(OH) 2 D had similar influenza disease as WT littermates. Influenza infection of T cell-specific Cyp27b1 KO resulted in increased respiratory distress and lethargy compared to WT littermates. WT and DIKO mice had more CD8+ T cells expressing higher amounts of granzyme B+ (GrzB) and disruptions in PD-1 than either whole-body Cyp27b1 KO or T-Cyp27b1 KO mice. Discussion The work suggests that renal 1,25(OH) 2 D is not required for host resistance to this influenza A virus infection. The work further demonstrates that immune cells and in particular T cells may produce 1,25(OH) 2 D to locally support CD8+ T cell responses to influenza A virus infection. Defining the mechanisms that regulate local 1,25(OH) 2 D production in T cells or other immune cell types is needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of immune cell-derived 1,25(OH) 2 D in host resistance to influenza infection, building on the background that immune cells express vitamin D receptors and the CYP27B1 enzyme, which are involved in producing the VDR ligand, 1,25(OH) 2 D.
Design and population
The study used a mouse model of H1N1 influenza A virus infection, examining various mouse strains including wild type (WT) littermates, whole-body Cyp27b1 knockout (Cyp27b1 KO) mice, mice lacking renal 1,25(OH) 2 D production (double intronic (DI)KO), mice with T cell-specific deletion of Cyp27b1 (T-Cyp27b1 KO), and mice with T cell-specific deletion of Vdr (T-Vdr KO).

Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that immune cells, particularly T cells, may produce 1,25(OH) 2 D locally to support CD8+ T cell responses to influenza A virus infection, highlighting a novel role for CYP27B1 in antiviral host defense.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the local regulation of 1,25(OH) 2 D production in immune cells during influenza infection, which could inform future studies on immune modulation and potential therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a mouse model, and the evidence is moderate as it does not fully address the role of 1,25(OH) 2 D in human influenza infections.

Risk of Hospitalization Related to Influenza A(H3N2) Virus Subclade K, Thailand, June-December 2025.

Authors: Kriengkrai Prasert, Prabda Praphasiri, Darunee Ditsungnoen, Suriya Naosri, Siripaporn Phuygun, Sutthichai Nakphook, Seesai Yeesoonsang, Jatuporn Saiyarin, Nuttida Thongpramul, Apinya Panjangampatthana
Original abstract (English)
Antigenically drifted influenza A(H3N2) subclade K viruses have contributed substantially to influenza burden since 2025, but their clinical severity is less well described. Using sentinel surveillance data from Thailand, we found no significant difference in the adjusted odds of hospitalization among those infected with subclade K versus other influenza viruses (adjusted odds ratio 1.06, 95% CI .67, 1.68). This approach to estimating hospitalization risk for an emerging influenza virus subclade using sentinel surveillance data could serve as a model for conducting future rapid assessments. Summary Using sentinel respiratory surveillance data from Thailand, this paper found no large, significant difference in hospitalization risk for patients infected with influenza A(H3N2) subclade K compared with other influenza viruses.

Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5Nx) viruses, particularly clade 2.3.4.4b, pose substantial global health risks. 季节性流感 疫苗接种 抗体反应的性别和年龄差异是由 B 细胞中 NF-κB 和 TNF 信号传导的雌激素上调介导的

Sex and age differences in antibody responses to seasonal influenza vaccination are mediated by estrogenic upregulation of NF-κB and TNF signaling in B cells
作者： Han-Sol Park, Anna Yin, Weiqiang Zhou, Eliane F. E. Wenstedt, Anne Jedlicka, Amanda Dziedzic, Annie Werner, Katherine Z. J. Fenstermacher, Huifen Li, Patrick J. Shea
摘要中文翻译摘要:分析了年轻人(YA;18-49 岁)中体液 免疫应答 与季节性四价流感 疫苗 (QIV) 或老年人(OA;75 岁以上)中高剂量 QIV 的性别差异,以确定年龄相关的变化(包括类固醇的变化)如何影响 B 细胞的性别差异。在 YAs 中,疫苗接种 (DPV) 后 28 天,女性的 H3N2 水平、中和抗体滴度、血凝素 (HA)+ CD19+ B 细胞和 HA+ 记忆 B 细胞的比例高于男性,但没有 H1N1,而在 OA 中未观察到这一点。机器学习算法表明,基线 (0 DPV) 类固醇,包括 17-羟基孕酮、雌激素和睾酮,以及 HA+ CD19+ B 细胞和 HA+ 抗体分泌 B 细胞 (ASC),是 28 DPV 时血清转化的主要预测因子,尤其是在 YA 中。单细胞 RNA 测序表明,YA 女性的 CD19+ B 细胞在 7 DPV 时比 YA 男性具有更高的转录活性,B 细胞亚群中雌激素反应元件 (ERE) 沿着 NF-κB 介导的 TNF 信号通路的基因上调,而在 OA 中这种情况有所减轻。来自 YA 女性(而非男性)的 ASC 中的雌二醇 治疗 增加了 HA+ IgG+ 细胞的数量和大小,以及 NF-κB 介导的 TNF 信号通路中 ERE 基因的表达,而雌激素受体拮抗剂可抑制这种情况。NF-κB 或 TNF 信号传导的药理抑制可阻断 E2 上调 YA 雌性细胞中抗体分泌的能力。这项研究提供了育龄女性中雌激素介导的流感 疫苗 诱导的抗体反应增加的机制见解,并表明雌激素信号在减少 疫苗 诱导的老年免疫性别差异中的作用。重要性随着年龄的增长,流感 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 的性别差异变得不那么明显,我们假设这可能与循环性腺类固醇的变化有关。我们的研究表明,在接受 季节性流感 疫苗 后,雌激素活性升高的年轻成年女性有更多识别流感血凝素的 B 细胞;他们的 B 细胞在雌激素信号传导和炎症通路上具有更强的活性,并且比年轻成年男性产生更强的抗体反应,而这些性别差异在老年人中得到了缓解。我们通过证明离体雌二醇通过雌激素受体和与 NF-κB 的结合增加 B 细胞的抗体产生,确定雌激素是流感免疫性别差异的关键驱动因素。这些发现有助于解释 疫苗 免疫力性别差异的生物学基础,并表明荷尔蒙环境,而不仅仅是实际年龄,决定了一个人对 疫苗 的反应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨年龄相关变化(包括激素波动)如何影响季节性流感 疫苗接种抗体应答中的性别差异,特别是在年轻成人和老年成人中。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 疫苗 后 28 天内年轻人(YA;18-49 岁)和老年人(OA;75 岁以上)对季节性四价流感 疫苗 (QIV) 的抗体反应。
核心方法
机器学习算法用于识别血清转化的预测因素,包括基线类固醇和 B 细胞标记物,而单细胞 RNA 测序则评估 B 细胞亚群中的转录活性和基因表达。
主要结果
在 YA 女性中,雌二醇 治疗 沿着 NF-κB 介导的 TNF 信号通路增加 HA+ IgG+ 细胞和 ERE 基因表达,这种作用被雌激素受体拮抗剂抑制。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,雌激素信号增强了 YA 女性的抗体分泌,并且由于荷尔蒙的变化,免疫应答 中的这些性别差异随着年龄的增长而减少。
科研与公卫意义
结果提供了对 疫苗 免疫性别差异背后的生物学机制的见解,并强调了在 疫苗 反应策略中考虑激素因素的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对年轻人和老年人的关注而受到限制,并且由于依赖离体模型且缺乏长期随访数据,证据不充分。

Sex and age differences in antibody responses to seasonal influenza vaccination are mediated by estrogenic upregulation of NF-κB and TNF signaling in B cells

Authors: Han-Sol Park, Anna Yin, Weiqiang Zhou, Eliane F. E. Wenstedt, Anne Jedlicka, Amanda Dziedzic, Annie Werner, Katherine Z. J. Fenstermacher, Huifen Li, Patrick J. Shea
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Sex differences in the humoral immune responses to the seasonal quadrivalent influenza vaccine (QIV) in young adults (YA; 18–49 years old) or high-dose QIV in old adults (OA; 75+ years old) were analyzed to determine how age-related changes, including in steroids, impact sex differences in B cells. Among YAs, females had greater H3N2, but not H1N1, neutralizing antibody titers, and greater proportions of hemagglutinin (HA)+ CD19+ B cells and HA+ memory B cells than males through 28 days post-vaccination (DPV), that was not observed among OAs. Machine learning algorithms illustrated that baseline (0 DPV) steroids, including 17-hydroxyprogesterone, estrogens, and testosterone, as well as HA+ CD19+ B cells and HA+ antibody-secreting B cells (ASCs), were major predictors of seroconversion at 28 DPV, particularly in YA. Single-cell RNA sequencing demonstrated that CD19+ B cells from YA females had greater transcriptional activity at 7 DPV than YA males, with upregulation of genes with estrogen-response elements (EREs) along NF-κB-mediated TNF signaling pathways in B-cell subsets, which was mitigated in OA. Estradiol treatment of ASCs from YA females, but not males, increased the number and size of HA+ IgG+ cells and expression of ERE genes along the NF-κB-mediated TNF signaling pathway,that was inhibited by an estrogen receptor antagonist. Pharmacological inhibition of either NF-κB or TNF signaling blocked the ability of E2 to upregulate antibody secretion in cells from YA females. This study provides mechanistic insights into estrogen-mediated increases in influenza vaccine-induced antibody responses among reproductive-aged females and suggests a role for estrogen signaling in the reduction of sex differences in vaccine-induced immunity with old age. IMPORTANCE Sex differences in influenza vaccine-induced immune responses become less pronounced with old age, which we hypothesize could be related to changes in circulating gonadal steroids. Our study shows that after receipt of the seasonal influenza vaccine, young adult females, who have elevated estrogenic activity, have more B cells that recognize influenza hemagglutinin; their B cells have greater activity along estrogen signaling and inflammatory pathways, and mount stronger antibody responses than young adult males, with these sex differences being mitigated in old adults. We identify estrogen as a key driver of sex differences in influenza immunity by showing that ex vivo estradiol increases antibody production by B cells through the estrogen receptor and engagement with NF-κB. These findings help explain the biological basis for sex differences in vaccine immunity and suggest that the hormonal environment, not just chronological age, shapes how well a person responds to vaccination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate how age-related changes, including hormonal fluctuations, influence sex differences in antibody responses to seasonal influenza vaccination, particularly in young adults and old adults.
Design and population
The study analyzed antibody responses to the seasonal quadrivalent influenza vaccine (QIV) in young adults (YA; 18–49 years old) and old adults (OA; 75+ years old) over 28 days post-vaccination.
Core methods
Machine learning algorithms were used to identify predictors of seroconversion, including baseline steroids and B-cell markers, while single-cell RNA sequencing assessed transcriptional activity and gene expression in B-cell subsets.
Main results
In YA females, estradiol treatment increased HA+ IgG+ cells and ERE gene expression along the NF-κB-mediated TNF signaling pathway, an effect inhibited by an estrogen receptor antagonist.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that estrogen signaling enhances antibody secretion in YA females, and that these sex differences in immune response diminish with old age due to hormonal changes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the biological mechanisms underlying sex differences in vaccine immunity and highlight the importance of considering hormonal factors in vaccine response strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on young and old adults, and the evidence is moderate due to the reliance on ex vivo models and the absence of long-term follow-up data.

79 岁男性感染流感后出现脑病、共济失调和严重功能衰退

A 79-Year-Old Man With Encephalopathy, Ataxia, and Severe Functional Decline After Influenza Infection
作者： Bilaal Hassan, Marisol Tarin, Shaquila Sarapao, Robert J. Balch, Hassan B, Tarin M, Sarapao S, Balch RJ., Bilaal S. Hassan, Robert Judson Balch
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨老年患者中 季节性流感 病毒的临床表现和潜在并发症,强调需要了解流感对易感人群神经和功能结果的影响。
设计与对象
这是一项单例研究,对象是一名 79 岁男性,他在感染流感后出现脑病、共济失调和严重功能衰退,研究于 2026 年 10 月至发表时在美国一家未指定的医疗中心进行。

科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,流感感染可能会导致老年人出现神经系统并发症,强调了在临床实践中监测和管理此类病例的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到单例设计的限制,可能无法推广,并且缺乏统计分析或比较数据,导致解释的可信度中等。

Question and background
The study aimed to explore the clinical manifestations and potential complications of seasonal influenza viruses in elderly patients, highlighting the need for understanding the impact of influenza on neurological and functional outcomes in vulnerable populations.
Design and population
This is a single-case study of a 79-year-old man who developed encephalopathy, ataxia, and severe functional decline following influenza infection, conducted at an unspecified medical center in the United States between October 2026 and the time of publication.
Core methods
The study involved clinical evaluation, including neurological and functional assessments, and a review of the patient's medical history and laboratory findings to determine the association between influenza infection and the observed symptoms.
Main results
The patient exhibited encephalopathy, ataxia, and significant functional decline after influenza infection, with no specific numerical data or statistical analysis provided in the evidence.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a rare but possible complication of influenza infection, emphasizing the need for further research into the neurological effects of seasonal influenza viruses in older adults.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that influenza infection may lead to neurological complications in elderly individuals, underscoring the importance of monitoring and managing such cases in clinical practice.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-case design, which may not be generalizable, and the absence of statistical analysis or comparative data, resulting in moderate confidence in the interpretation.

髓系细胞中维生素D受体(VDR)和甲状旁腺激素(PTH)的表达调控小鼠对流感病毒感染的宿主反应

Myeloid cell expression of the VDR and parathyroid hormone control the host response to influenza infection in mice
作者： Tang M, Froelich N, Hanna N, Weaver V, Nader G, Sutton T, Cantorna M
摘要中文翻译通过 VDR 的维生素 D 信号传导可导致感染消退,而缺乏 VDR 则会导致过度炎症、IFNβ 和 IFNγ 升高以及肺部病理恶化。随后的实验没有动力将性别作为一个变量进行检查,这是该研究的局限性。使用2^−ΔΔCT方法计算相对基因表达水平,以GAPDH作为管家基因。使用组织培养感染剂量 50% (TCID50) 测定对感染性病毒进行定量,这是一种根据细胞培养物中的细胞病变效应确定感染性病毒浓度的标准方法。数值为平均值±SEM,代表两到三个独立实验的汇总结果(WT:n = 36;VDR KO:总共 n = 28)。简介 季节性流感 病毒引起的呼吸道感染仍然是全球主要的健康负担。维生素D受体(VDR)信号传导调节免疫应答,但VDR及其与甲状旁腺激素(PTH)的相互作用在宿主抵抗H1N1流感感染中的作用尚不清楚。方法将全局VDR敲除(KO)小鼠,或髓性(M)VDR缺失(M-VDR KO)和各自的野生型(WT)同窝仔鼠感染H1N1流感病毒。其他实验用间歇性 PTH 或媒介物治疗 WT 小鼠,然后用 H1N1 甲型流感病毒感染它们。评估疾病严重程度、死亡率、病毒负荷和清除、肺部病理学和炎症细胞因子反应。结果WT小鼠感染H1N1流感导致非致死性疾病,而VDR KO小鼠死亡率为21%,病毒清除延迟,症状和肺部病理学更严重。呼吸系统疾病严重程度的增加伴随着肺部 I 型和 II 型干扰素反应的升高。M-VDR KO 小鼠疾病严重程度增加...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨维生素 D 受体 (VDR) 和甲状旁腺激素 (PTH) 在调节 宿主 对流感感染反应中的作用,特别关注疾病严重程度、宿主 和炎症细胞因子反应。
设计与对象
该研究使用 C57BL/6 VDR 敲除 (KO) 和野生型 (WT) 同窝小鼠来评估 VDR 和 PTH 对流感感染的影响。小鼠感染甲型H1N1流感病毒,监测其临床症状、体重减轻和呼吸评分。
核心方法
该研究涉及使用补充表 S1 中引物的 PCR 进行基因分型、通过比色测定进行血清钙测量、使用 NanoDrop 分光光度计评估 RNA 浓度和纯度,以及用于流感病毒 M 基因定量的定量 PCR (qRT-PCR)。使用 TCID50 测定法测量感染性病毒,并使用血细胞计数器和台盼蓝评估细胞活力。
主要结果
与 WT 同窝小鼠相比,VDR KO 小鼠表现出 21% 的 死亡率、病毒清除延迟、更严重的症状和肺部病理。与媒介物处理的对照组相比,PTH 治疗 增加了 WT 小鼠的临床评分、流感 M 基因水平和细胞因子反应。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为一种新范例的证据,其中维生素 D-PTH 轴通过调节肺部炎症和病毒载量来调节 宿主 对流感病毒疾病的抵抗力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,VDR 和 PTH 通路可能是理解和潜在调节 宿主 对流感感染反应的重要靶点,为炎症控制和病毒清除机制提供见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于它使用小鼠作为模型生物,这可能无法完全转化为人类流感感染。由于依赖动物模型且缺乏直接的人体临床数据,证据强度中等。

Myeloid cell expression of the VDR and parathyroid hormone control the host response to influenza infection in mice

Authors: Tang M, Froelich N, Hanna N, Weaver V, Nader G, Sutton T, Cantorna M
Original abstract (English)
Vitamin D signaling via VDR leads to resolution of infection, while absence of VDR results in hyper-inflammation, elevated IFNβ and IFNγ, and worsened lung pathology. Subsequent experiments were not powered to examine sex as a variable which is a limitation of the study. Relative gene expression levels were calculated using the 2^−ΔΔCT method, with GAPDH as the housekeeping gene. Infectious virus was quantified using the Tissue Culture Infectious Dose 50% (TCID50) assay, a standard method for determining the concentration of infectious virus based on cytopathic effect in cell culture. Values are the mean ± SEM and represent pooled results from two to three independent experiments (WT: n = 36; VDR KO: n = 28 total). IntroductionRespiratory infections caused by seasonal influenza viruses remain a major global health burden. Vitamin D receptor (VDR) signaling regulates immune responses, but the role of the VDR and its interaction with parathyroid hormone (PTH) in host resistance to H1N1 influenza infection is not understood.MethodsGlobal VDR knockout (KO) mice, or myeloid (M) deletion of the VDR (M-VDR KO) and the respective wildtype (WT) littermates were infected with H1N1 influenza virus. Additional experiments treated WT mice with intermittent PTH or vehicle and then infected them with a H1N1 influenza A virus. Disease severity, mortality, viral burden and clearance, lung pathology, and inflammatory cytokine responses were evaluated.ResultsH1N1 influenza infection of WT mice resulted in non-lethal disease, whereas VDR KO mice had 21% mortality, delayed viral clearance, more severe symptoms and lung pathology. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of vitamin D receptor (VDR) and parathyroid hormone (PTH) in modulating the host response to influenza infection, particularly focusing on disease severity, mortality, and inflammatory cytokine responses.
Design and population
The study used C57BL/6 VDR knockout (KO) and wild-type (WT) littermates to evaluate the effects of VDR and PTH on influenza infection. Mice were infected with H1N1 influenza virus, and their clinical symptoms, weight loss, and respiratory scores were monitored.
Core methods
The study involved genotyping using PCR with primers from Supplementary Table S1, serum calcium measurement via a colorimetric assay, RNA concentration and purity assessment using a NanoDrop spectrophotometer, and quantitative PCR (qRT-PCR) for influenza virus M gene quantitation. Infectious virus was measured using the TCID50 assay, and cell viability was assessed with a hemocytometer and trypan blue.
Main results
VDR KO mice showed 21% mortality, delayed viral clearance, more severe symptoms, and lung pathology compared to WT littermates. PTH treatment increased clinical scores, influenza M gene levels, and cytokine responses in WT mice compared to vehicle-treated controls.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as evidence of a novel paradigm where the vitamin D-PTH axis regulates host resistance to influenza virus disease by modulating inflammation and viral load in the lungs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that VDR and PTH pathways could be important targets for understanding and potentially modulating host responses to influenza infection, offering insights into inflammation control and viral clearance mechanisms.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its use of mice as the model organism, which may not fully translate to human influenza infections. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on animal models and the absence of direct human clinical data.

高致病性禽流感H5N1的山羊乳腺感染模型

A caprine mammary infection model for highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1
作者： Aubrey L, Berube N, Pessoa N, Swan C, Javed M, Bertens C, Scruten E, Stevens R, Norleen C, Cutts K
摘要中文翻译处理接种腺体后,工作人员佩戴的手套用 Prevail 消毒。研究设计:基因型 D1.1 病毒接种 三只山羊在病毒攻击前适应 7 天。在研究开始之前,在牛奶中病毒排出高峰期的 4 DPI (D4) 时,预先选择了一只山羊进行安乐死,这种情况之前已在奶牛中发现过 (20)。其余两只山羊在牛奶中脱落的传染性病毒解决后,在 19 DPI (D19) 静脉注射 Dorminal(20 ml 每 45 kg 体重;Rafter 8 Products Inc.)处死。研究设计:疫苗接种、B3.13 病毒攻击和再攻击 7 只山羊适应 7 天。在 D35,所有七只山羊的右乳腺均受到 104 TCID50 基因型 B3.13 病毒的攻击,如“研究设计:基因型 D1.1 病毒接种”部分所述。五个 DPC,在研究第 40 天,对 4 号山羊实施安乐死,以在牛奶中病毒排出高峰期间收集组织。如“研究设计:基因型 D1.1 病毒接种”部分所述,在二次攻击后对山羊进行 10 天的监测,并在 D73 时实施安乐死。高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 病毒 跨物种溢出 进入奶牛体内,代表出现了能够维持大流行的哺乳动物 宿主,并创造了一条 传播 传播给人类的途径。涉及奶牛的高致病性禽流感研究受到在高度生物防护中饲养大型动物所需的空间要求的限制。小型反刍动物模型可以实现更大的样本量,同时降低成本、提高安全性并降低进行研究的后勤障碍。在这里,我们建立了奶山羊作为 HPAI 乳腺感染的模型。乳房内接种 HPAI H5N1 基因型 D1.1 和基因型 B3.1...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发山羊乳腺感染模型,以研究高致病性禽流感 H5N1 的 传播 和 免疫应答,满足对相关动物模型研究病毒 传播 和牲畜疾病进展的需求。
设计与对象
该研究涉及七只山羊,经过 7 天的适应,其中三只山羊在颈部皮下注射疫苗,三只只接受佐剂。每只接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的 队列研究 都有一台挤奶机,所有程序均在 CL3 实验室进行。
核心方法
山羊接种 HPAI H5N1 基因型 D1.1 和 B3.13,并使用 TCID50 测定法测量病毒载量。收集血清和乳汁样本进行病毒中和和 ELISA 测试,并根据直肠温度和呼吸、抑郁和乳腺炎体征进行临床评分。
主要结果
乳房内接种 HPAI H5N1 基因型 D1.1 和 B3.13 会导致急性乳腺炎、产奶量下降和牛奶中病毒大量脱落。与仅使用佐剂的组相比,疫苗 组的临床症状和病毒脱落有所减少。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为该模型在研究 H5N1 发病机制 和 免疫应答 中的实用性的证据,强调了其在 疫苗 开发和功效测试中的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 疫苗 的开发和对牲畜中 H5N1 传播 的理解提供了信息,这对于防止人畜共患传播和确保食品安全至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和使用单一动物物种的限制,但结果得到了直接观察和经过验证的方案的支持,为研究结果提供了适度的可信度。

A caprine mammary infection model for highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1

Authors: Aubrey L, Berube N, Pessoa N, Swan C, Javed M, Bertens C, Scruten E, Stevens R, Norleen C, Cutts K
Original abstract (English)
Gloves worn by personnel were disinfected with Prevail after handling the inoculated gland.Study design: Genotype D1.1 virus inoculationThree goats were acclimated for 7 days before viral challenge. Before the study was initiated, one goat was preselected to be euthanized at 4 DPI (D4) during the period of peak viral shedding in milk, which had previously been identified in dairy cows (20). The remaining two goats were euthanized at 19 DPI (D19) by intravenous injection with Dorminal (20 ml per 45 kg of body weight; Rafter 8 Products Inc.) after resolution of infectious virus shedding in milk.Study design: Vaccination, B3.13 virus challenge, and rechallengeSeven goats were acclimated for 7 days. Five DPC, at study day 40, goat #4 was euthanized for tissue collection during the period of peak viral shedding in milk. Goats were monitored for 10 days post–secondary challenge and were euthanized at D73, as described in the “Study design: Genotype D1.1 virus inoculation” section. Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 virus spillover into dairy cows represents the emergence of a mammalian host capable of sustaining a panzootic and creates a transmission pathway to humans. HPAI research involving dairy cows is constrained by the space requirements needed to house large animals in high biocontainment. A small ruminant model would enable larger sample sizes while reducing costs, improving safety, and lowering logistical barriers to conduct research. Here, we establish dairy goats as a model for HPAI mammary infection. Intramammary inoculation with HPAI H5N1 genotype D1.1 and genotype B3.1...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a caprine mammary infection model to investigate the pathogenesis and immune response to highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1, addressing the need for a relevant animal model to study viral transmission and disease progression in livestock.
Design and population
The study involved seven goats acclimated for 7 days, with three vaccinated subcutaneously in the neck and three receiving adjuvant alone. One milking machine was dedicated to each vaccinated and unvaccinated cohort, and all procedures were conducted in CL3 laboratories.
Core methods
Goats were inoculated with HPAI H5N1 genotypes D1.1 and B3.13, and viral load was measured using TCID50 assays. Serum and milk samples were collected for virus neutralization and ELISA tests, with clinical scoring based on rectal temperatures and signs of respiration, depression, and mastitis.
Main results
Intramammary inoculation with HPAI H5N1 genotype D1.1 and B3.13 resulted in acute mastitis, decreased milk yield, and high virus shedding in milk. The vaccine group showed reduced clinical signs and viral shedding compared to the adjuvant-only group.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence of the model's utility in studying H5N1 pathogenesis and immune response, highlighting its potential for vaccine development and efficacy testing.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of vaccines and the understanding of H5N1 transmission in livestock, which is critical for preventing zoonotic spread and ensuring food safety.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and the use of a single animal species, but the results are supported by direct observations and validated protocols, providing moderate confidence in the findings.

多克隆母乳来源的分泌型 IgA 赋予 抗病毒药物 广泛的针对多种呼吸道病毒的保护作用

Polyclonal breast milk–derived secretory IgA confers broad antiviral protection against multiple respiratory viruses
作者： Tugg Y, Ang J, Ahmed I, Mehta R, Mane V, Afkhami S, Miller M
摘要中文翻译这些发现凸显了母乳来源的分泌型 IgA 作为一种可扩展的、针对粘膜的被动免疫策略的潜力,能够应对地方性和新出现的呼吸道病毒威胁。使用先前经过验证并获得专利的纯化方法 (13) 对大约 1 升混合牛奶进行处理,产生多克隆 SIgA 产品,命名为 LCTG-001。摘要被动免疫是保护原发性免疫缺陷个体免受传染病侵害的重要工具,并为保护弱势群体提供快速反应策略,特别是在病原体出现和 疫苗 部署之间的间隔期间。目前的方法依赖于静脉内输送富含 IgG 的血浆或单克隆抗体,这限制了粘膜表面(呼吸道病毒的主要入口点)的可及性、可扩展性和有效性。在这里,我们测试了 LCTG-001,一种源自母乳混合的多克隆分泌型 IgA 制剂,以解决这些局限性。LCTG-001 对临床相关呼吸道病毒(包括呼吸道合胞病毒、腺病毒和甲型流感病毒)表现出广泛的 抗病毒药物 活性。LCTG-001 有效识别 1 组和 2 组甲型流感亚型,包括高致病性 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b,并参与非中和效应机制,例如神经氨酸酶抑制和中性粒细胞激活。当鼻内给药时,LCTG-001在免疫功能正常和抗体缺陷的小鼠模型中提供了针对甲型流感攻击的完全保护,通过短暂的早期病毒负荷减少以及随后的肺部病理学和肺屏障完整性的持续改善来预防发病和死亡率。材料一...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过测试 LCTG-001(一种源自人母乳的多克隆分泌型 IgA 制剂)对抗临床相关呼吸道病毒(包括甲型流感病毒)来解决当前 抗病毒药物 保护方法的局限性,以提供广泛的 抗病毒药物 保护。
设计与对象
该研究涉及从 120 名哺乳期个体的母乳中收集和纯化 LCTG-001,并在细胞培养物和中性粒细胞中测试其对呼吸道合胞病毒、腺病毒和甲型流感病毒的抵抗力。
核心方法
使用细胞培养物和中性粒细胞纯化 LCTG-001 并测试 抗病毒药物 活性。这些方法包括 SDS-聚丙烯酰胺凝胶电泳、密度梯度离心和 传播 电子显微镜用于病毒纯化和定量。
