LCTG-001 对呼吸道合胞病毒、腺病毒和甲型流感病毒表现出广泛的 抗病毒药物 活性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 LCTG-001 提供的广泛 抗病毒药物 保护的新证据,这可能有助于预防多种呼吸道病毒。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为预防呼吸道感染的新 抗病毒药物 策略的制定提供信息,特别是针对婴儿和老年人等弱势群体。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用体外模型以及需要进一步的临床验证。由于该研究的体外性质,证据强度中等。

Polyclonal breast milk–derived secretory IgA confers broad antiviral protection against multiple respiratory viruses

Authors: Tugg Y, Ang J, Ahmed I, Mehta R, Mane V, Afkhami S, Miller M
Original abstract (English)
These findings highlight the potential of breast milk–derived secretory IgA as a scalable, mucosa-targeted passive immunization strategy capable of addressing both endemic and emerging respiratory viral threats. Approximately 1 liter of this pooled milk was processed using a previously validated and patented purification method (13) to yield a polyclonal SIgA product, designated LCTG-001. AbstractPassive immunization is an important tool for protecting individuals with primary immune deficiencies from infectious diseases and offers a rapid response strategy for protecting vulnerable populations, particularly during the interval between pathogen emergence and vaccine deployment. Current approaches rely on intravenous delivery of IgG-enriched plasma or monoclonal antibodies, which limits accessibility, scalability, and effectiveness at mucosal surfaces, the primary entry point for respiratory viruses. Here, we tested LCTG-001, a polyclonal secretory IgA preparation derived from pooled human breast milk, to address these limitations. LCTG-001 demonstrated broad antiviral activity against clinically relevant respiratory viruses, including respiratory syncytial virus, adenovirus, and influenza A virus. LCTG-001 effectively recognized both group 1 and group 2 influenza A subtypes, including the highly pathogenic H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b, and engaged nonneutralizing effector mechanisms such as neuraminidase inhibition and neutrophil activation. When administered intranasally, LCTG-001 conferred complete protection against influenza A challenge in both immunocompetent and antibody-deficient murine models, preventing morbidity and mortality through a transient early reduction in viral burden followed by sustained improvements in lung pathology and lung barrier integrity. MATERIALS A...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address limitations in current antiviral protection methods by testing LCTG-001, a polyclonal secretory IgA preparation derived from human breast milk, against clinically relevant respiratory viruses, including influenza A virus, to provide broad antiviral protection.
Design and population
The study involved the collection and purification of LCTG-001 from the breast milk of 120 lactating individuals and testing it against respiratory syncytial virus, adenovirus, and influenza A virus in cell cultures and neutrophils.
Core methods
LCTG-001 was purified and tested for antiviral activity using cell cultures and neutrophils. The methods included SDS–polyacrylamide gel electrophoresis, density gradient centrifugation, and transmission electron microscopy for virus purification and quantification.
Main results
LCTG-001 demonstrated broad antiviral activity against respiratory syncytial virus, adenovirus, and influenza A virus.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as novel evidence of broad antiviral protection provided by LCTG-001, which could be useful for preventing multiple respiratory viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could inform the development of new antiviral strategies for preventing respiratory infections, particularly in vulnerable populations such as infants and the elderly.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of in vitro models and the need for further clinical validation. The evidence strength is moderate due to the in vitro nature of the study.

蝙蝠传播的甲型 H9N2 流感病毒显示物种特异性复制和 传播

Bat-borne influenza A H9N2 virus shows species-specific replication and transmission
作者： Jan Schinköthe, Jacob Schön, Lorenz Ulrich, Andrea Aebischer, Reiner Ulrich, Annika Graaf, Anne Balkema‐Buschmann, Ghazi Kayali, Kevin Ciminski, Martin Schwemmle
摘要中文翻译蝙蝠传播的 H9N2 IAV (bat H9N2) 最初于 2017 年在埃及的蝙蝠粪便中检测到,最近因其意想不到的大流行前特性而引起关注,包括雪貂之间的空气传播 传播 以及在人类肺外植体培养物中的有效复制。然而,其 宿主 范围和潜在的 宿主 宿主 的定义仍然不明确,特别是对于其他蝙蝠 宿主 和哺乳动物或鸟类混合容器物种。在这里,我们比较了两种翼足类蝙蝠物种 Rousettus aegyptiacus 和 Eidolon helvum 的敏感性,并另外评估了鼻内接种蝙蝠 H9N2 后的猪和鹌鹑。埃及伊蚊仅表现出低水平的病毒复制和脱落,很大程度上局限于上呼吸道,并且没有表现出传播接触蝙蝠。相比之下,幼年和成年的鼠疫,尽管没有表现出任何临床症状,但在上呼吸道中表现出高水平的病毒复制和脱落,并能有效且早期地接触蝙蝠。病毒RNA在呼吸道鼻甲、鼻咽道和气管上皮中最为丰富,与中度至重度坏死性鼻炎和气管炎有关。下呼吸道受累较少,病毒RNA局限于分散的细支气管,并与局灶性坏死性细支气管炎相关。其他器官,包括胃肠道,缺乏组织学病变和病毒 RNA 标记。在猪和鹌鹑中,鼻内接种不会导致可检测到的复制、传播 或血清转化。我们的研究结果表明,所测试的两种果蝠物种之间对蝙蝠 H9N2 的易感性存在显着差异,并将 E. helvum 鉴定为高度易感的 宿主,能够快速有效地进行 传播。相反,猪和鹌鹑不受影响,这表明可能需要额外的病毒适应才能在这些物种中产生有效的感染。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查蝙蝠传播的 H9N2 甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 的 宿主 范围和 传播 潜力,因为它显示出意想不到的大流行前特性,包括雪貂之间空气传播的 传播 以及在人肺外植体培养物中的有效复制。
设计与对象
该研究比较了两种翼足类蝙蝠(Rousettus aegyptiacus 和 Eidolon helvum)的敏感性,并评估了猪和鹌鹑鼻内接种蝙蝠 H9N2 后的情况。
核心方法
研究人员对动物进行鼻内接种,通过检查各组织中的病毒RNA水平和观察临床症状来评估病毒复制、脱落和传播。
主要结果
Rousettus aegyptiacus 仅表现出低水平的病毒复制和脱落,主要局限于上呼吸道,并且没有表现出 传播 来接触蝙蝠。相比之下,幼年和成年 Eidolon helvum 均在上呼吸道中表现出高水平的病毒复制和脱落,并能有效且早期地接触蝙蝠。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,所测试的两种果蝠物种之间对蝙蝠 H9N2 的易感性存在显着差异,并将 Eidolon helvum 确定为高度易感的 宿主,能够快速有效地进行 传播。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了了解蝙蝠传播的 H9N2 IAV 的 宿主 范围对于 监测 和风险评估的重要性,特别是在识别潜在的 储存宿主 宿主 和混合容器物种方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于少量动物的体内实验,不包括长期监测或更广泛的 宿主 物种,但由于对复制、脱落和 传播 的直接观察,证据有力。

Bat-borne influenza A H9N2 virus shows species-specific replication and transmission

Authors: Jan Schinköthe, Jacob Schön, Lorenz Ulrich, Andrea Aebischer, Reiner Ulrich, Annika Graaf, Anne Balkema‐Buschmann, Ghazi Kayali, Kevin Ciminski, Martin Schwemmle
Original abstract (English)
Bat-borne H9N2 IAV (bat H9N2), initially detected in bat feces in Egypt in 2017, has recently attracted attention because of its unexpected pre-pandemic properties, including airborne transmission between ferrets and efficient replication in human lung explant cultures. However, its host range and potential reservoir hosts remain poorly defined, particularly with respect to additional bat hosts and mammalian or avian mixing-vessel species. Here, we compare the susceptibility of two pteropodid bat species, Rousettus aegyptiacus and Eidolon helvum, and additionally assessed pigs and quails following intranasal inoculation with bat H9N2. aegyptiacus showed only low-level viral replication and shedding, largely restricted to the upper respiratory tract, and did not show transmission to contact bats. In contrast, both juvenile and adult E. helvum, despite showing no clinical signs, showed high-level viral replication and shedding in the upper respiratory tract, with efficient and early transmission to contact bats. Viral RNA was most abundant in the respiratory turbinates, the nasopharyngeal meatus and the tracheal epithelium, where it was associated with moderate to severe necrotizing rhinitis and tracheitis. The lower respiratory tract was less involved, with viral RNA confined to scattered bronchioles and associated with focal necrotizing bronchiolitis. Other organs, including the gastrointestinal tract, lacked histologic lesions and viral RNA labeling. In pigs and quails, intranasal inoculation did not result in detectable replication, transmission, or seroconversion. Our findings demonstrate marked differences in the susceptibility to bat H9N2 between the two fruit bat species tested and identify E. helvum as a highly susceptible host capable of rapid and efficient transmission. Conversely, pigs and quails were not susceptible, suggesting that additional viral adaptations may be required to allow for a productive infection in these species.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the host range and transmission potential of bat-borne H9N2 influenza A virus (IAV), as it has shown unexpected pre-pandemic properties, including airborne transmission between ferrets and efficient replication in human lung explant cultures.
Design and population
The study compared the susceptibility of two pteropodid bat species, Rousettus aegyptiacus and Eidolon helvum, and also assessed pigs and quails following intranasal inoculation with bat H9N2.
Core methods
The researchers intranasally inoculated the animals and assessed viral replication, shedding, and transmission through examination of viral RNA levels in various tissues and observation of clinical signs.
Main results
Rousettus aegyptiacus showed only low-level viral replication and shedding, largely restricted to the upper respiratory tract, and did not show transmission to contact bats. In contrast, both juvenile and adult Eidolon helvum showed high-level viral replication and shedding in the upper respiratory tract, with efficient and early transmission to contact bats.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate marked differences in susceptibility to bat H9N2 between the two fruit bat species tested and identify Eidolon helvum as a highly susceptible host capable of rapid and efficient transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the importance of understanding the host range of bat-borne H9N2 IAV for surveillance and risk assessment, particularly in identifying potential reservoir hosts and mixing-vessel species.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vivo experiments with a small number of animals and does not include long-term monitoring or broader host species, but the evidence is strong due to direct observations of replication, shedding, and transmission.

西班牙血清型 4 侵袭性肺炎球菌病的谱系动态和危险因素。

Lineage Dynamics and Risk Factors Underlying Serotype 4 Invasive Pneumococcal Disease in Spain.
作者： Covadonga Pérez-García, Joaquin Llorente, Maria Elena Aguirre Alastuey, Mirella Llamosí, Ruth Gil-Prieto, Gabriel Laghlali, Farah El-Ayache, Vivian Yan, Michael Schotsaert, Jorge Del Diego-Salas
摘要中文翻译背景:疫苗 覆盖血清型的出现导致侵袭性肺炎球菌疾病 (IPD) 是全世界范围内的一个严重问题。方法:我们对西班牙肺炎球菌参考实验室(2009-2024 年)收到的所有来自儿童和成人的国家血清型 4 IPD 分离株进行了特征分析。结果:我们调查了 COVID-19 大流行后导致年轻人 IPD 的血清型 4 意外增加,近年来影响了老年人群。流行病学和基因组分析证实,由于 GPSC12 谱系中的 ST15063,该病例的上升始于 2022 年塞维利亚(安达卢西亚)的突然聚集性病例。该 暴发 最初影响与高吸烟率、酗酒和吸入药物相关的未接种疫苗的年轻人,随后在接下来的几年中在全国范围内广泛传播,影响了 65 岁以上的成年人。此外,ST15063 血清型 4 菌株显示出人类肺细胞的感染率增强,在暴露于香烟烟雾和流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 合并感染的情况下,感染率显着增加。结论:这些发现强调需要针对高危人群采取针对性的 疫苗接种 策略、分子 监测 和重点干预措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
疫苗 覆盖血清型的出现导致侵袭性肺炎球菌疾病 (IPD) 是全世界范围内的一个严重问题。
设计与对象
我们对西班牙肺炎球菌参考实验室(2009-2024 年)收到的所有来自儿童和成人的国家血清型 4 IPD 分离株进行了特征分析。
核心方法
我们调查了 COVID-19 大流行后导致年轻人 IPD 的血清型 4 意外增加,近年来影响了老年人群。
主要结果
流行病学和基因组分析证实,由于 GPSC12 谱系中的 ST15063,该病例的上升始于 2022 年塞维利亚(安达卢西亚)的突然聚集性病例。
解释与创新
该 暴发 最初影响与高吸烟率、酗酒和吸入药物相关的未接种疫苗的年轻人,随后在接下来的几年中在全国范围内广泛传播,影响了 65 岁以上的成年人。
科研与公卫意义
此外,ST15063 血清型 4 菌株显示出人类肺细胞的感染率增强,在暴露于香烟烟雾和流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 合并感染的情况下,感染率显着增加。
局限与证据强度
这些发现强调需要有针对性的 疫苗接种 策略、分子 监测 以及针对高危人群的针对性干预措施。

Lineage Dynamics and Risk Factors Underlying Serotype 4 Invasive Pneumococcal Disease in Spain.

Authors: Covadonga Pérez-García, Joaquin Llorente, Maria Elena Aguirre Alastuey, Mirella Llamosí, Ruth Gil-Prieto, Gabriel Laghlali, Farah El-Ayache, Vivian Yan, Michael Schotsaert, Jorge Del Diego-Salas
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The emergence of vaccine-covered serotypes causing invasive pneumococcal disease (IPD) is a serious concern worldwide. METHODS: We characterized all national serotype 4 IPD isolates from children and adults received at the Spanish Pneumococcal Reference Laboratory (2009-2024). RESULTS: We investigated the unexpected rise of serotype 4 causing IPD in young adults after the COVID-19 pandemic, affecting the elderly population in recent years. Epidemiological and genomic analysis confirmed that the rise started as an abrupt cluster of cases in Seville (Andalusia) in the year 2022 due to the ST15063 within the GPSC12 lineage. This outbreak initially affected nonvaccinated young individuals associated with high rates of tobacco smoking, alcohol, and inhaled drugs followed by a general distribution through the country in the following years that affected adults ≥65 years old. Moreover, ST15063 serotype 4 strains displayed enhanced infection rates of human lung cells that significantly increased in the presence of cigarette smoke exposure and by influenza A(H3N2) coinfection. CONCLUSIONS: These findings highlight the need for targeted vaccination strategies, molecular surveillance, and focused interventions in high-risk populations.

The emergence of vaccine-covered serotypes causing invasive pneumococcal disease (IPD) is a serious concern worldwide.
Design and population

Core methods

Main results
Epidemiological and genomic analysis confirmed that the rise started as an abrupt cluster of cases in Seville (Andalusia) in the year 2022 due to the ST15063 within the GPSC12 lineage.
Interpretation and novelty
This outbreak initially affected nonvaccinated young individuals associated with high rates of tobacco smoking, alcohol, and inhaled drugs followed by a general distribution through the country in the following years that affected adults ≥65 years old.
Scientific and public-health significance
Moreover, ST15063 serotype 4 strains displayed enhanced infection rates of human lung cells that significantly increased in the presence of cigarette smoke exposure and by influenza A(H3N2) coinfection.
Limitations and evidence strength

多尿嘧啶束的破坏可以大大增强流感 delVG 的免疫刺激特性

Disruption of the polyuracil tract can greatly enhance immunostimulatory properties of influenza delVGs
作者： Sydney NZ Jordan, Marisa Mendes, Alistair B Russell
摘要中文翻译非标准病毒 基因组 (nsVG) 是病毒群体中无法复制的 基因组,其存在与增强的 免疫应答 相关。这种关联引起了人们对它们作为潜在的 抗病毒药物、疫苗 佐剂或一般免疫调节剂的兴趣。在甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 中,主要的 nsVG 是三个聚合酶片段之一的大缺失,从而产生包含缺失的病毒 基因组 (delVG)。我们小组的工作发现,可以恢复能够刺激高水平干扰素的个体 delVG,但是我们和其他人之前尚未找到任何一致的方法来预测或修改这种刺激的决定因素。使用高通量遗传方法,我们发现破坏 IAV delVG 中多尿嘧啶束的特定 突变 可以将干扰素产量提高多达两个数量级。这些变体还可以与旨在排除传染性病毒的人工系统中改进的救援相关,并且可以极大地抑制具有复制能力的病毒的生长。这些发现表明,多尿嘧啶束是改善源自 IAV 的免疫刺激性 delVG 的关键且意想不到的靶标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解为什么某些非标准病毒 基因组 (nsVG) 会增强 免疫应答 以及如何预测或修改这些特性。nsVG 与增强型 免疫应答 的结合引发了人们对其作为 抗病毒药物、疫苗 佐剂或一般免疫调节剂的潜在用途的兴趣。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用高通量遗传学方法来研究含有甲型流感病毒(IAV)缺失的病毒基因组(delVGs)及其免疫刺激特性。
核心方法
采用高通量遗传方法来恢复和分析个体 delVG,重点关注破坏多尿嘧啶束以增强干扰素产生的特定 突变。
主要结果
研究发现,破坏 IAV delVG 中多尿嘧啶束的特定 突变 可将干扰素产量提高两个数量级。这些变体还显示出对人工系统的改善的救援作用,并抑制了具有复制能力的病毒的生长。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,多尿嘧啶束是改善 IAV 衍生的 delVG 免疫刺激特性的关键靶点,为增强 免疫应答 提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为新的 抗病毒药物 策略和 疫苗 佐剂的开发提供信息,从而有可能改善 免疫应答 对抗流感感染的效果。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于高通量遗传方法,并没有提供对所涉及机制的完整理解。样本量和对照没有明确详细说明,这可能会限制结果的普遍性。

Disruption of the polyuracil tract can greatly enhance immunostimulatory properties of influenza delVGs

Authors: Sydney NZ Jordan, Marisa Mendes, Alistair B Russell
Original abstract (English)
Non-standard viral genomes (nsVGs) are replication-incompetent genomes within viral populations whose presence has been associated with an enhanced immune response. This association has garnered interest in their use as potential antivirals, vaccine adjuvants, or general immune-modulators. In influenza A virus (IAV), the predominant nsVG is a large deletion in one of the three polymerase segments, which results in deletion-containing viral genomes (delVGs). Work from our group has found that individual delVGs can be recovered that are capable of stimulating high levels of interferon, however we and others have not previously found any consistent means of predicting or modifying determinants of this stimulation. Using high-throughput genetic approaches, we find that specific mutations disrupting the polyuracil tract in IAV delVGs can enhance interferon production by up to two orders of magnitude. Such variants can also associate with improved rescue in artificial systems engineered to exclude infectious virus, and can greatly suppress the growth of replication-competent virus. These findings indicate the polyuracil tract is a key, and unexpected, target for improvement of immunostimulatory delVGs derived from IAV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand why certain non-standard viral genomes (nsVGs) enhance immune responses and how to predict or modify these properties. The association of nsVGs with enhanced immune responses has sparked interest in their potential use as antivirals, vaccine adjuvants, or general immune-modulators.
Design and population
The study involved the use of high-throughput genetic approaches to investigate influenza A virus (IAV) deletion-containing viral genomes (delVGs) and their immunostimulatory properties.
Core methods
High-throughput genetic approaches were employed to recover and analyze individual delVGs, focusing on specific mutations disrupting the polyuracil tract to enhance interferon production.
Main results
Specific mutations disrupting the polyuracil tract in IAV delVGs were found to enhance interferon production by up to two orders of magnitude. These variants also showed improved rescue in artificial systems and suppressed the growth of replication-competent virus.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that the polyuracil tract is a key target for improving the immunostimulatory properties of delVGs derived from IAV, providing a novel approach to enhance immune responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results could inform the development of new antiviral strategies and vaccine adjuvants, potentially improving the immune response against influenza infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's findings are based on high-throughput genetic approaches and do not provide a complete understanding of the mechanisms involved. The sample size and controls are not explicitly detailed, which may limit the generalizability of the results.

蝙蝠甲型流感病毒的进化重建揭示了片段特异性历史和 宿主 相关选择

Evolutionary reconstruction of bat influenza A viruses reveals segment-specific histories and host-associated selection
作者： Austen Rowell, Matthew G. Johnson, David A. Rasmussen, Lisa M. Bono
摘要中文翻译摘要 甲型流感 (IAV) 是一种主要的人畜共患病原体,感染多种脊椎动物 宿主。在这项研究中,我们重点推断从新大陆蝙蝠中分离出来的新型 IAV 子集的进化史,这些 IAV 使用非规范的 宿主 受体,并推断它们与唾液酸结合 IAV 的关系。与唾液酸结合的循环菌株相比,人们对这些新亚型何时出现以及选择如何作用于它们知之甚少。该领域目前的状态存在分歧,即蝙蝠是否是天然的 储存宿主(针对独特但低丰度的 IAV 谱系),或者流感是否在最近来自其标准 宿主 范围内的物种的 跨物种溢出 后经历了快速 进化。通过分析特定片段的系统发育,我们希望阐明蝙蝠 IAV 的进化历史。我们的目标是推断非规范蝙蝠 IAV 与唾液酸结合 IAV 的分歧日期,与唾液酸结合 IAV 的代表性样本相比,识别作用于蝙蝠 IAV 的选择模式,以确定 基因组 广泛适应的潜在特征,最后重建祖先状态转换以推断哪个 宿主 分类单元先于 跨物种溢出 进入蝙蝠。为了实现这一目标,我们使用最小二乘分子测年、贝叶斯离散特征祖先 宿主 重建和分支位点选择测试独立分析了每个 IAV 基因组 片段。我们的结果表明,非典型蝙蝠 IAV 在 100-200 年前就与主要 IAV 谱系分离,支持了更深层的进化历史,而不是最近的 跨物种溢出 事件。然而,基因组 片段之间在推断的分歧时间、选择压力和祖先 宿主 方面的不一致表明,蝙蝠 IAV 经历了片段特异性的进化历史。总之,这些发现表明,非典型蝙蝠 IAV 代表了一个截然不同的谱系,具有复杂的进化历史,涉及片段特异性选择和 宿主 过渡动力学。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感 (IAV) 是一种主要的人畜共患病原体,感染多种脊椎动物 宿主。与唾液酸结合的循环菌株相比,人们对这些新亚型何时出现以及选择如何作用于它们知之甚少。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,我们重点推断从新大陆蝙蝠中分离出来的新型 IAV 子集的进化史,这些 IAV 使用非规范的 宿主 受体,并推断它们与唾液酸结合 IAV 的关系。
核心方法
为了实现这一目标,我们使用最小二乘分子测年、贝叶斯离散特征祖先 宿主 重建和分支位点选择测试独立分析了每个 IAV 基因组 片段。
主要结果
我们的结果表明,非典型蝙蝠 IAV 在 100-200 年前就与主要 IAV 谱系分离,支持了更深层的进化历史,而不是最近的 跨物种溢出 事件。
解释与创新
然而,基因组 片段之间在推断的分歧时间、选择压力和祖先 宿主 方面的不一致表明,蝙蝠 IAV 经历了片段特异性的进化历史。总之,这些发现表明,非典型蝙蝠 IAV 代表了一个截然不同的谱系,具有复杂的进化历史,涉及片段特异性选择和 宿主 过渡动力学。
科研与公卫意义
通过分析特定片段的系统发育,我们希望阐明蝙蝠 IAV 的进化历史。这些发现可能为 监测 针对流感病毒的策略提供信息,并有助于了解 IAV 中 宿主 转变和进化适应的动态。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或分析方法的具体局限性。

Evolutionary reconstruction of bat influenza A viruses reveals segment-specific histories and host-associated selection

Authors: Austen Rowell, Matthew G. Johnson, David A. Rasmussen, Lisa M. Bono
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Influenza A (IAV) is a major zoonotic pathogen infecting a broad range of vertebrate hosts. In this study, we focus on inferring the evolutionary history of a subset of novel IAV, isolated from New World bats, which use a non-canonical host receptor and infer their relationship to sialic-acid-binding IAV. Little is known about when these novel subtypes emerged and how selection has acted upon them compared to sialic-acid-binding circulating strains. The current state of the field is split on whether bats are natural reservoirs for a distinct yet low abundance IAV lineage, or if influenza underwent rapid evolution after a recent spillover from a species within its standard host range. By analyzing segment-specific phylogenies, we hope to clarify the evolutionary history of bat IAV. Our objectives are to infer the date of divergence of non-canonical bat IAV from sialic-acid binding IAV, identify patterns of selection acting on bat IAV compared to representative samples of sialic-acid-binding IAV to identify potential signatures of genome-wide adaptation, and finally to reconstruct ancestral state transitions to infer which host taxa preceded spillover into bats. To accomplish this, we analyzed each IAV genome segment independently using least-squares molecular dating, Bayesian discrete-trait ancestral host reconstruction, and branch-site selection tests. Our results suggest that non-canonical bat IAV diverged from major IAV lineages 100-200 years ago, supporting a deeper evolutionary history rather than a very recent spillover event. However, discordance among genome segments in inferred divergence times, selective pressures, and ancestral hosts indicates that bat IAV experienced segment-specific evolutionary histories. Together, these findings suggest that non-canonical bat IAV represents a deeply divergent lineage with a complex evolutionary history involving segment-specific selection and host-transition dynamics.
View research seven-element analysis

Core methods
To accomplish this, we analyzed each IAV genome segment independently using least-squares molecular dating, Bayesian discrete-trait ancestral host reconstruction, and branch-site selection tests.
Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Together, these findings suggest that non-canonical bat IAV represents a deeply divergent lineage with a complex evolutionary history involving segment-specific selection and host-transition dynamics.

By analyzing segment-specific phylogenies, we hope to clarify the evolutionary history of bat IAV. These findings may inform surveillance strategies for influenza viruses and help understand the dynamics of host transitions and evolutionary adaptation in IAV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design or analysis methods.

混合性肺炎合并肺栓塞和支气管肺泡灌洗液中检测到的多种病原体:A 病例报告。

Mixed Pneumonia with Pulmonary Embolism and Multiple Pathogens Detected in Bronchoalveolar Lavage Fluid: A Case Report.
作者： Xia Chen
摘要中文翻译严重的社区获得性肺炎可能涉及混合病原体并并发血管并发症。一名 72 岁男性出现发烧、干咳、呕吐、低氧血症、炎症标志物显着升高以及 D-二聚体浓度升高。胸部计算机断层扫描显示左下叶实变和胸腔积液。由于低氧血症和疑似肺栓塞而进行的计算机断层扫描肺血管造影显示右上叶肺动脉充盈缺损。患者在支气管肺泡灌洗(BAL)之前接受了抗菌治疗。在与放射学病变相对应的支气管肺段进行支气管镜检查,并无菌回收约10 mL的支气管肺泡灌洗液(BALF)用于微生物学和细胞学检测。将至少 3 mL 的等分试样分配给多重病原体靶向测序测定,该测定检测鹦鹉热衣原体、甲型 H3N2 流感、肺炎链球菌和其他条件致病性或共生生物。结合临床表现、影像学、炎症标志物、BALF 细胞学、常规测试和 治疗 反应来解释这三个主要检测结果。该患者在使用抗菌剂和抗凝剂 治疗 期间病情有所改善,并于 2025 年 12 月 3 日出院。在 12 月 17 日记录的门诊随访中,利伐沙班又持续了大约一个月。该案例凸显了靶向测序在治疗患者中的辅助价值和解释局限性。连续成像和侵入性采样反映了针对具体病例的决定,不应被解释为肺炎的常规方案。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
严重的社区获得性肺炎可能涉及混合病原体并并发血管并发症。该研究旨在强调靶向测序对治疗患者的辅助价值和解释局限性。
设计与对象
对一名 72 岁男性进行了 病例报告 治疗,该男性出现发烧、干咳、呕吐、低氧血症、炎症标志物显着升高以及 D-二聚体浓度升高。该研究涉及连续成像和侵入性采样,以评估临床表现和诊断结果。
核心方法
将至少 3 mL 的支气管肺泡灌洗液 (BALF) 等分试样分配给多重病原体靶向测序测定。该检测检测到了鹦鹉热衣原体、甲型H3N2流感、肺炎链球菌和其他条件致病性或共生生物。
主要结果
计算机断层扫描肺血管造影显示右上叶肺动脉充盈缺损。靶向测序分析确定甲型 H3N2 流感、鹦鹉热衣原体和肺炎链球菌为三种主要病原体。
解释与创新
研究结果结合临床表现、影像学、炎症标志物、BALF 细胞学、常规测试和 治疗 反应进行解释。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调了考虑重症肺炎混合病原体的重要性以及靶向测序在识别多种病原体方面的价值。它还强调在复杂的临床情况下需要综合诊断方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于单个 病例报告,这限制了普遍性。连续成像和侵入性采样反映了针对具体病例的决定,不应被解释为肺炎的常规方案。

Mixed Pneumonia with Pulmonary Embolism and Multiple Pathogens Detected in Bronchoalveolar Lavage Fluid: A Case Report.

Authors: Xia Chen
Original abstract (English)
Severe community-acquired pneumonia may involve mixed pathogens and concurrent vascular complications. A 72-year-old man presented with fever, nonproductive cough, vomiting, hypoxemia, markedly elevated inflammatory markers, and an elevated D-dimer concentration. Chest computed tomography showed left lower-lobe consolidation and pleural effusion. Computed tomography pulmonary angiography, obtained because of hypoxemia and suspected pulmonary embolism, showed a filling defect in a right upper-lobe pulmonary artery. The patient received antimicrobial therapy prior to bronchoalveolar lavage (BAL). Bronchoscopy was performed in the bronchopulmonary segment corresponding to the radiographic lesion, and approximately 10 mL of bronchoalveolar lavage fluid (BALF) was recovered aseptically for microbiological and cytological testing. An aliquot of at least 3 mL was allocated to a multiplex pathogen-targeted sequencing assay, which detected Chlamydia psittaci, influenza A H3N2, Streptococcus pneumoniae, and additional conditionally pathogenic or commensal organisms. The three principal detections were interpreted in conjunction with the clinical presentation, imaging, inflammatory markers, BALF cytology, conventional testing, and treatment response. The patient improved during antimicrobial and anticoagulant treatment and was discharged on December 3, 2025. At the documented outpatient follow-up on December 17, rivaroxaban was continued for approximately one further month. This case highlights the adjunctive value and interpretive limitations of targeted sequencing in a treated patient. The serial imaging and invasive sampling reflected case-specific decisions and should not be interpreted as a routine protocol for pneumonia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Severe community-acquired pneumonia may involve mixed pathogens and concurrent vascular complications. The study aimed to highlight the adjunctive value and interpretive limitations of targeted sequencing in a treated patient.
Design and population
A case report was conducted on a 72-year-old man who presented with fever, nonproductive cough, vomiting, hypoxemia, markedly elevated inflammatory markers, and an elevated D-dimer concentration. The study involved serial imaging and invasive sampling to assess the clinical presentation and diagnostic findings.
Core methods
An aliquot of at least 3 mL of bronchoalveolar lavage fluid (BALF) was allocated to a multiplex pathogen-targeted sequencing assay. The assay detected Chlamydia psittaci, influenza A H3N2, Streptococcus pneumoniae, and additional conditionally pathogenic or commensal organisms.
Main results
Computed tomography pulmonary angiography showed a filling defect in a right upper-lobe pulmonary artery. The targeted sequencing assay identified influenza A H3N2, Chlamydia psittaci, and Streptococcus pneumoniae as the three principal pathogens.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings were interpreted in conjunction with clinical presentation, imaging, inflammatory markers, BALF cytology, conventional testing, and treatment response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The case underscores the importance of considering mixed pathogens in severe pneumonia and the value of targeted sequencing in identifying multiple causative agents. It also emphasizes the need for integrated diagnostic approaches in complex clinical scenarios.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a single case report, which limits generalizability. The serial imaging and invasive sampling reflected case-specific decisions and should not be interpreted as a routine protocol for pneumonia.

基质金属蛋白酶-8缺乏改变H1N1感染中病毒清除与免疫病理之间的平衡。

Matrix Metalloproteinase-8 Deficiency Alters the Balance Between Viral Clearance and Immunopathology in H1N1 Infection.
作者： Xiaoyun Wang, Joselyn Rojas-Quintero, Duo Zhang, Joaquin Zuñiga, Caroline A Owen
摘要中文翻译基质金属蛋白酶 8 (MMP-8) 调节炎症反应,但其对甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 疾病 发病机制 的贡献尚不清楚。在感染 H1N1 或季节性 IAV 的受试者中,血浆 MMP-8 水平增加了 30 倍,并且与 PaO2/FiO2 比率呈负相关,这表明作为肺损伤严重程度的候选标志物的潜在价值,需要在更大的 队列研究 中进行验证。感染 H1N1 的野生型 (WT) 小鼠肺部 MMP-8 水平升高,并定位于气道上皮细胞、巨噬细胞和多形核中性粒细胞 (PMN)。H1N1 感染的 Mmp-8-/- 小鼠表现出较低的 死亡率 和肺部病毒负荷减少,但比 WT 小鼠出现更严重的肺损伤。基因表达谱显示,受感染的 Mmp-8-/- 小鼠肺部坏死性凋亡增强,免疫应答 增强。受感染的 Mmp-8-/- 小鼠气道上皮坏死性凋亡增加,肺部 I 型干扰素 (IFN) 水平升高,表达 II 类 MHC 分子的巨噬细胞和 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞的频率增加,以及一些 Th1 细胞因子水平升高。在体外,MMP-8 裂解 IL-1β 和 IL-6,但不裂解 I 型 IFN。这些发现表明,Mmp-8 缺乏与流感病毒传播受限和肺损伤增加有关,并伴有 I 型 IFN 相关坏死性凋亡信号增强和 抗病毒药物 免疫应答 增强。后者可能部分涉及 MMP-8 介导的促炎介质加工过程的丧失。MMP-8 可能代表严重流感感染中的 宿主 反应靶标,尽管针对 MMP-8 的治疗策略需要在增强的病毒清除率与增加的肺部免疫病理学风险之间取得平衡。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基质金属蛋白酶 8 (MMP-8) 调节炎症反应,但其对甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 疾病 发病机制 的贡献尚不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究涉及感染 H1N1 的野生型 (WT) 小鼠和 Mmp-8-/- 小鼠,测量了感染 H1N1 或季节性 IAV 的受试者的血浆 MMP-8 水平。
核心方法
感染 H1N1 的 WT 小鼠肺部 MMP-8 水平升高,并定位于气道上皮细胞、巨噬细胞和多形核中性粒细胞 (PMN)。
主要结果
H1N1 感染的 Mmp-8-/- 小鼠表现出较低的 死亡率 和肺部病毒负荷减少,但比 WT 小鼠出现更严重的肺损伤。基因表达谱显示,受感染的 Mmp-8-/- 小鼠肺部坏死性凋亡增强,免疫应答 增强。
解释与创新
受感染的 Mmp-8-/- 小鼠气道上皮坏死性凋亡增加,肺部 I 型干扰素 (IFN) 水平升高,表达 II 类 MHC 分子的巨噬细胞和 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞的频率增加,以及一些 Th1 细胞因子水平升高。MMP-8 可能代表严重流感感染中的 宿主 反应靶标,尽管针对 MMP-8 的治疗策略需要在增强的病毒清除率与增加的肺部免疫病理学风险之间取得平衡。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,Mmp-8 缺乏与流感病毒传播受限和肺损伤增加有关,并伴有 I 型 IFN 相关坏死性凋亡信号增强和 抗病毒药物 免疫应答 增强。MMP-8可能是严重流感感染治疗干预的潜在靶点。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到使用单一流感毒株的限制,并且缺乏 MMP-8 作为生物标志物或治疗靶点的直接临床验证。研究结果表明,流感 发病机制 中 MMP-8 和 免疫应答 之间存在复杂的相互作用,但需要进一步研究在更大的 队列研究 和人体研究中证实这些结果。

Matrix Metalloproteinase-8 Deficiency Alters the Balance Between Viral Clearance and Immunopathology in H1N1 Infection.

Authors: Xiaoyun Wang, Joselyn Rojas-Quintero, Duo Zhang, Joaquin Zuñiga, Caroline A Owen
Original abstract (English)
Matrix metalloproteinase-8 (MMP-8) regulates inflammatory responses, but its contributions to the pathogenesis of influenza A viral (IAV) disease are not known. Plasma MMP-8 levels were increased 30-fold in subjects infected with H1N1 or seasonal IAV and were inversely associated with the PaO2/FiO2 ratio, suggesting potential value as a candidate marker of lung injury severity that requires validation in larger cohorts. MMP-8 levels were elevated in the lungs of H1N1-infected wild-type (WT) mice and localized to airway epithelial cells, macrophages, and polymorphonuclear neutrophils (PMNs). H1N1-infected Mmp-8-/- mice exhibited lower mortality and reduced lung viral burdens, but developed more severe lung injury than WT mice. Gene expression profiling revealed enhanced necroptosis and an exaggerated immune response in the lungs of infected Mmp-8-/- mice. Infected Mmp-8-/- mice had increased airway epithelial necroptosis with higher pulmonary levels of Type I interferons (IFNs), and increased frequencies of Class II MHC molecule-expressing macrophages and CD4+ and CD8+ T cells, and higher levels of some Th1 cytokines. In vitro, MMP-8 cleaved IL-1β and IL-6, but not Type I IFNs. These findings suggest that Mmp-8 deficiency is associated with restricted influenza virus spread and increased lung injury, accompanied by enhanced Type I IFN-associated necroptotic signaling and augmented antiviral immune responses. The latter may partly involve loss of MMP-8-mediated processing of pro-inflammatory mediators. MMP-8 may represent a host-response target in severe influenza infection, although therapeutic strategies targeting MMP-8 will need to balance enhanced viral clearance against the risk of increased lung immunopathology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Matrix metalloproteinase-8 (MMP-8) regulates inflammatory responses, but its contributions to the pathogenesis of influenza A viral (IAV) disease are not known.
Design and population
The study involved H1N1-infected wild-type (WT) mice and Mmp-8-/- mice, with plasma MMP-8 levels measured in subjects infected with H1N1 or seasonal IAV.
Core methods
MMP-8 levels were elevated in the lungs of H1N1-infected WT mice and localized to airway epithelial cells, macrophages, and polymorphonuclear neutrophils (PMNs).
Main results
H1N1-infected Mmp-8-/- mice exhibited lower mortality and reduced lung viral burdens, but developed more severe lung injury than WT mice. Gene expression profiling revealed enhanced necroptosis and an exaggerated immune response in the lungs of infected Mmp-8-/- mice.
Interpretation and novelty
Infected Mmp-8-/- mice had increased airway epithelial necroptosis with higher pulmonary levels of Type I interferons (IFNs), and increased frequencies of Class II MHC molecule-expressing macrophages and CD4+ and CD8+ T cells, and higher levels of some Th1 cytokines. MMP-8 may represent a host-response target in severe influenza infection, although therapeutic strategies targeting MMP-8 will need to balance enhanced viral clearance against the risk of increased lung immunopathology.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that Mmp-8 deficiency is associated with restricted influenza virus spread and increased lung injury, accompanied by enhanced Type I IFN-associated necroptotic signaling and augmented antiviral immune responses. MMP-8 may be a potential target for therapeutic intervention in severe influenza infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a single influenza strain and the absence of direct clinical validation of MMP-8 as a biomarker or therapeutic target. The findings suggest a complex interplay between MMP-8 and immune responses in influenza pathogenesis, but further research is needed to confirm these results in larger cohorts and in human studies.

乙型流感神经氨酸酶抗体对症状性流感病毒感染的保护作用。

Protective Effects of Influenza B Neuraminidase Antibodies Against Symptomatic Influenza Virus Infection.
作者： Skyler Rogers, Kelsey M Sumner, Yangyupei Yang, Emileigh Johnson, Ran Balicer, Alon Peretz, David Greenberg, Avital Hirsch, Mark A Katz, Florian Krammer
摘要中文翻译背景:流感疫苗疫苗旨在诱导血凝素抑制(HAI)抗体,而非神经氨酸酶抑制(NAI)抗体;因此,需要进一步研究NAI抗体的保护作用。对于流感A病毒,神经氨酸酶抑制抗体的保护作用已被证明,但尚未在流感B病毒(IBVs)中得到证实。方法:我们采用H6NB重配病毒作为靶标,在以色列的医疗工作者(HCP)中进行前瞻性流感疫苗接种有效性研究,评估NAI抗体对IBV的保护作用。通过HAI和NAI试验定量测定针对B/Brisbane/60/2008和B/Phuket/3073/2013的抗体滴度。分析了2017-2018流感季节基线和疫苗接种后血清中匹配的NAI和HAI抗体滴度。结果:实验室确认的IBV感染在64/262名HCP中被记录,而流感疫苗接种感染在187/262名HCP中被记录。疫苗接种并未一致诱导针对疫苗株B/Brisbane的NAI滴度上升,几何均数滴度上升为1.1(95% CI:.9-1.3)。然而,与未接种疫苗的HCP相比,接种疫苗的HCP针对B/Brisbane的基线NAI滴度几何均数滴度比为1.50(95% CI:1.0-2.1),针对流行B/Phuket的几何均数滴度比为2.5(95% CI:1.7-3.6)。在调整HAI滴度后,针对B/Phuket的NAI抗体滴度每上升2倍,症状性感染的危险性降低42%(95% CI:36%-48%),针对B/Brisbane的危险性降低23%(95% CI:13%-33%)。结论:神经氨酸酶抑制抗体是针对症状性IBV感染的强保护相关因素,其保护作用独立于血凝素抑制抗体的作用。这些发现表明,在流感疫苗中使用和标准化神经氨酸酶成分可能提供更广泛的流感保护。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感 疫苗 旨在诱导血凝素抑制 (HAI) 抗体,而不是神经氨酸酶抑制 (NAI) 抗体;因此,NAI抗体的保护作用有待进一步研究。
设计与对象
神经氨酸酶抑制抗体的保护作用(独立于 HAI 抗体)已在甲型流感病毒中得到证实,但在乙型流感病毒 (IBV) 中尚未得到证实。
核心方法
我们采用 H6NB 重配病毒靶标,通过一项针对以色列医护人员 (HCP) 的前瞻性流感 疫苗 有效性研究,评估了 NAI 抗体对血清中 IBV 的保护作用。
主要结果
64/262 例 HCP 中记录了实验室确诊的 IBV 感染,187/262 例 HCP 中记录了流感 疫苗接种。
解释与创新
对 2017-2018 年流感季节的基线和疫苗接种后血清中匹配的 NAI 和 HAI 抗体滴度进行了分析。
科研与公卫意义
根据 HAI 滴度进行调整,NAI 抗体滴度每上升 2 倍,与 B/普吉岛相比,有症状感染的风险就会降低 42%(95% CI:36%-48%),与 B/布里斯班相比,会降低 23%(95% CI:13%-33%)。
局限与证据强度
神经氨酸酶抑制抗体与针对症状性 IBV 感染的保护作用密切相关,其保护作用独立于 HAI 抗体的保护作用。

Protective Effects of Influenza B Neuraminidase Antibodies Against Symptomatic Influenza Virus Infection.

Authors: Skyler Rogers, Kelsey M Sumner, Yangyupei Yang, Emileigh Johnson, Ran Balicer, Alon Peretz, David Greenberg, Avital Hirsch, Mark A Katz, Florian Krammer
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Influenza vaccines are designed to induce hemagglutinin-inhibiting (HAI) antibodies rather than neuraminidase-inhibiting (NAI) antibodies; therefore, NAI antibodies' protective effects need further investigation. Neuraminidase-inhibiting antibodies' protective effects, independent of HAI antibodies, have been demonstrated for influenza A viruses, but not influenza B viruses (IBVs). METHODS: Employing H6NB reassortant virus targets, we evaluated NAI antibodies' protective effects against IBV in sera from a prospective influenza vaccine effectiveness study among healthcare personnel (HCP) in Israel. Antibody titers against B/Brisbane/60/2008 and B/Phuket/3073/2013 were quantified by HAI and NAI assays. Matched NAI and HAI antibody titers from baseline and postvaccination sera were analyzed for the 2017-2018 influenza season. RESULTS: Laboratory-confirmed IBV infection was recorded in 64/262 HCP and influenza vaccination in 187/262 HCP. Vaccination did not consistently elicit rising NAI titers against vaccine strain B/Brisbane, with a geometric mean fold rise of 1.1 (95% CI: .9-1.3). However, comparing baseline NAI titers, geometric mean titer ratios were 1.50 (95% CI: 1.0-2.1) times higher against B/Brisbane and 2.5 (95% CI: 1.7-3.6) times higher against circulating B/Phuket in vaccinated versus unvaccinated HCP. Each 2-fold rise in NAI antibody titers reduced the hazard of symptomatic infection by 42% (95% CI: 36%-48%) against B/Phuket and 23% (95% CI: 13%-33%) against B/Brisbane, adjusting for HAI titers. CONCLUSIONS: Neuraminidase-inhibiting antibodies were a strong correlate of protection against symptomatic IBV infection, with protective effects independent of those from HAI antibodies. These findings suggest that the use and standardization of neuraminidase content in influenza vaccines could provide broader protection against influenza.

Main results

与未感染 HIV 的成人相比,三价灭活流感 疫苗 对 HIV 感染者的免疫原性和安全性:来自中国的一项研究

Immunogenicity and safety of trivalent inactivated influenza vaccine in adults living with HIV compared with HIV-uninfected adults: a study from China
作者： Jinfeng Su, Kai Gao, Shasha Han, Xuewen Tang, Xue Guo, Hui Liang, Xiaoqin Liu, Xu Xiaoteng, Jun Yan, Ye Qiu
摘要中文翻译目的 评价三价灭活流感 疫苗 (IIV3) 在中国 18 岁以上成人 HIV 感染者中的免疫原性和安全性,为中国该人群感染 疫苗 流感提供证据。方法 这项单中心、开放标签的比较分析源自一项更大规模的三组临床研究。在本次比较分析中,计划有 472 名接种疫苗的参与者,其中包括 236 名感染艾滋病毒的成年人(第 1 组)和 236 名年龄和性别相当的未感染艾滋病毒的成年人(第 2 组)。两组参与者均在第 0 天接受一剂 IIV3 (0.5 mL)。在 疫苗接种 之前和之后 28 天收集血样,用于针对 A(H1N1)、甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 毒株的血凝抑制 (HI) 抗体测试。免疫原性终点包括血清转化率、血清保护率、几何平均滴度(GMT)和几何平均增加(GMI)。通过在 6 个月的随访期间监测主动和主动不良事件 (AE) 以及严重不良事件 (SAE) 来评估安全性。原始试验中未接种疫苗的 队列研究 成人 HIV 感染者仅用于对流感样疾病 (ILI) 进行探索性、描述性分析,未评估 疫苗 的疗效。结果 共有 489 名参与者入组,其中第 1 组 237 名,第 2 组 252 名。疫苗接种 后 28 天,A(H1N1) 组 1 和组 2 之间的血清转换率没有显着差异(91.00% vs. 86.69%,P > 0.05),但 甲型H3N2流感病毒 的血清转换率显着较低(49.50% 对比 89.52%)和 B/维多利亚(40.00% 对比 64.52%)(均 P < 0.001)。A(H1N1)、甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 的血清保护率在第 1 组中也低于第 2 组(分别为 94.00% vs. 97.18% 和 40.50% vs. 68.55%)。第 1 组中所有三种菌株的 疫苗接种 后 GMT 均显着较低(所有 P < 0.001)。第 1 组中 A(H1N1) 的 GMI 较高,但 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 的 GMI 低于第 2 组(所有 P < 0.001)。多变量分析表明,在调整人口和临床因素(特别是 甲型H3N2流感病毒)后,HIV 感染仍然与 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 的多项测量值独立相关。IIV3 通常是安全且耐受性良好的。大多数 AE 的严重程度较轻,没有报告 4 级不良反应或 疫苗 相关的 SAE。在 疫苗接种 后 29 天至 6 个月期间,接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的 HIV 感染者中几乎没有报告流感样疾病事件;这些发现是描述性的,并不用于评估 疫苗 对流感的功效。结论 成人 HIV 感染者在 IIV3 疫苗接种 后产生了显着的抗体反应,尽管反应的程度因流感毒株而异。在感染 HIV 的成年人中,A(H1N1) 血清转化率较高,而对 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 的反应通常低于未感染 HIV 的成年人。在多变量调整后,HIV 感染仍然与 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 减少有关,对于几种毒株特异性结果,甲型H3N2流感病毒 的关联尤其明显。IIV3 安全且耐受性良好,支持当前针对成人 HIV 感染者流感 疫苗接种 的建议。临床试验注册https://www.chictr.org.cn/showprojEN.html?proj=255613,标识符ChiCTR2500112191。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估三价灭活流感 疫苗 (IIV3) 在成人 HIV 感染者中的免疫原性和安全性,并为中国该人群中流感 疫苗 的支持提供证据。
设计与对象
这项单中心、开放标签的比较分析源自一项更大规模的三组临床研究。它包括 472 名接种疫苗的参与者,其中 236 名感染艾滋病毒的成年人(第 1 组)和 236 名年龄和性别相当的未感染艾滋病毒的成年人(第 2 组)。
核心方法
在 疫苗接种 之前和之后 28 天收集血样,用于针对 A(H1N1)、甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 毒株的血凝抑制 (HI) 抗体测试。通过在 6 个月的随访期间监测主动和主动不良事件 (AE) 以及严重不良事件 (SAE) 来评估安全性。
主要结果
共有 489 名参与者入组,其中第 1 组 237 名,第 2 组 252 名。在 疫苗接种 后 28 天,A(H1N1) 组 1 和组 2 之间的血清转换率没有显着差异(91.00% vs. 86.69%,P > 0.05),但 甲型H3N2流感病毒 的血清转换率显着较低(49.50% 对比 89.52%)和 B/维多利亚(40.00% 对比 64.52%)(均 P < 0.001)。
解释与创新
在多变量调整后,HIV 感染与 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 减少独立相关,对于几种毒株特异性结果,甲型H3N2流感病毒 的关联尤其明显。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持目前针对成人 HIV 感染者的流感 疫苗接种 的建议,因为 IIV3 通常是安全的且耐受性良好。
局限与证据强度
该研究是单中心分析,结果可能无法推广到其他人群。由于该研究的观察性质以及缺乏对所有潜在混杂因素的控制,证据是中等的。

Immunogenicity and safety of trivalent inactivated influenza vaccine in adults living with HIV compared with HIV-uninfected adults: a study from China

Authors: Jinfeng Su, Kai Gao, Shasha Han, Xuewen Tang, Xue Guo, Hui Liang, Xiaoqin Liu, Xu Xiaoteng, Jun Yan, Ye Qiu
Original abstract (English)
Objective To evaluate the immunogenicity and safety of trivalent inactivated influenza vaccine (IIV3) in adults aged ≥18 years living with HIV and to provide evidence supporting influenza vaccination in this population in China. Methods This single-center, open-label comparative analysis was derived from a larger three-arm clinical study. For the present comparative analysis, 472 vaccinated participants were planned, including 236 adults living with HIV (Group 1) and 236 age- and sex-comparable HIV-uninfected adults (Group 2). Participants in both groups received one dose of IIV3 (0.5 mL) on Day 0. Blood samples were collected before and 28 days after vaccination for hemagglutination inhibition (HI) antibody testing against A(H1N1), A(H3N2), and B/Victoria strains. Immunogenicity endpoints included seroconversion rate, seroprotection rate, geometric mean titer (GMT), and geometric mean increase (GMI). Safety was assessed by monitoring solicited and unsolicited adverse events (AEs) and serious adverse events (SAEs) during 6 months of follow-up. An unvaccinated cohort of adults living with HIV from the original trial was included only for an exploratory, descriptive analysis of influenza-like illness (ILI), without assessment of vaccine efficacy. Results A total of 489 participants were enrolled, including 237 in Group 1 and 252 in Group 2. At 28 days post-vaccination, seroconversion rates did not differ significantly between Group 1 and Group 2 for A(H1N1) (91.00% vs. 86.69%, P > 0.05), but significantly lower for A(H3N2) (49.50% vs. 89.52%) and B/Victoria (40.00% vs. 64.52%) (both P < 0.001). Seroprotection rates were also lower in Group 1 than in Group 2 for A(H1N1), A(H3N2), and B/Victoria (94.00% vs. 97.18%, and 40.50% vs. 68.55%, respectively). Post-vaccination GMTs were significantly lower in Group 1 for all three strains (all P < 0.001). GMI was higher for A(H1N1) but lower for A(H3N2) and B/Victoria in Group 1 than in Group 2 (all P < 0.001). Multivariable analyses showed that HIV infection remained independently associated with several measures of vaccine-induced immune responses after adjustment for demographic and clinical factors, particularly for A(H3N2). IIV3 was generally safe and well tolerated. Most AEs were mild in severity, and no Grade 4 adverse reactions or vaccine-related SAEs were reported. During the period from 29 days to 6 months after vaccination, few influenza-like illness events were reported among vaccinated and unvaccinated participants living with HIV; these findings were descriptive and were not used to assess vaccine efficacy against influenza. Conclusion Adults living with HIV developed substantial antibody responses following IIV3 vaccination, although the magnitude of response varied across influenza strains. A(H1N1) seroconversion was high in adults living with HIV, whereas responses to A(H3N2) and B/Victoria were generally lower than those in HIV-uninfected adults. HIV infection remained associated with reduced vaccine-induced immune responses for several strain-specific outcomes after multivariable adjustment, with associations particularly evident for A(H3N2). IIV3 was safe and well tolerated, supporting current recommendations for influenza vaccination in adults living with HIV. Clinical trial registration https://www.chictr.org.cn/showprojEN.html?proj=255613 , identifier ChiCTR2500112191.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the immunogenicity and safety of trivalent inactivated influenza vaccine (IIV3) in adults living with HIV and to provide evidence supporting influenza vaccination in this population in China.
Design and population
This single-center, open-label comparative analysis was derived from a larger three-arm clinical study. It included 472 vaccinated participants, with 236 adults living with HIV (Group 1) and 236 age- and sex-comparable HIV-uninfected adults (Group 2).
Core methods
Blood samples were collected before and 28 days after vaccination for hemagglutination inhibition (HI) antibody testing against A(H1N1), A(H3N2), and B/Victoria strains. Safety was assessed by monitoring solicited and unsolicited adverse events (AEs) and serious adverse events (SAEs) during 6 months of follow-up.
Main results
A total of 489 participants were enrolled, including 237 in Group 1 and 252 in Group 2. At 28 days post-vaccination, seroconversion rates did not differ significantly between Group 1 and Group 2 for A(H1N1) (91.00% vs. 86.69%, P > 0.05), but significantly lower for A(H3N2) (49.50% vs. 89.52%) and B/Victoria (40.00% vs. 64.52%) (both P < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
HIV infection remained independently associated with reduced vaccine-induced immune responses for several strain-specific outcomes after multivariable adjustment, with associations particularly evident for A(H3N2).
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support current recommendations for influenza vaccination in adults living with HIV, as IIV3 was generally safe and well tolerated.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a single-center analysis, and the results may not be generalizable to other populations. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the lack of control for all potential confounding factors.

多克隆母乳来源的分泌型 IgA 赋予 抗病毒药物 广泛的针对多种呼吸道病毒的保护作用

Polyclonal breast milk–derived secretory IgA confers broad antiviral protection against multiple respiratory viruses
作者： Yona Tugg, Jann Catherine Ang, Imran Ahmed, Rikin Mehta, Viraj P. Mane, Sam Afkhami, Matthew S. Miller
摘要中文翻译被动免疫是保护原发性免疫缺陷个体免受传染病侵害的重要工具,并为保护弱势群体提供快速反应策略,特别是在病原体出现和 疫苗 部署之间的间隔期间。目前的方法依赖于静脉内输送富含 IgG 的血浆或单克隆抗体,这限制了粘膜表面(呼吸道病毒的主要入口点)的可及性、可扩展性和有效性。在这里,我们测试了 LCTG-001,一种源自母乳混合的多克隆分泌型 IgA 制剂,以解决这些局限性。LCTG-001 对临床相关呼吸道病毒(包括呼吸道合胞病毒、腺病毒和甲型流感病毒)表现出广泛的 抗病毒药物 活性。LCTG-001 有效识别 1 组和 2 组甲型流感亚型,包括高致病性 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b,并参与非中和效应机制,例如神经氨酸酶抑制和中性粒细胞激活。当鼻内给药时,LCTG-001在免疫功能正常和抗体缺陷的小鼠模型中提供了针对甲型流感攻击的完全保护,通过短暂的早期病毒负荷减少以及随后的肺部病理学和肺屏障完整性的持续改善来预防发病和死亡率。这些发现凸显了母乳来源的分泌型 IgA 作为一种可扩展的、针对粘膜的被动免疫策略的潜力,能够应对地方性和新出现的呼吸道病毒威胁。

问题与背景
被动免疫是保护原发性免疫缺陷个体免受传染病侵害的重要工具,并为保护弱势群体提供快速反应策略,特别是在病原体出现和 疫苗 部署之间的间隔期间。

目前的方法依赖于静脉内输送富含 IgG 的血浆或单克隆抗体,这限制了粘膜表面(呼吸道病毒的主要入口点)的可及性、可扩展性和有效性。

科研与公卫意义
当鼻内给药时,LCTG-001在免疫功能正常和抗体缺陷的小鼠模型中提供了针对甲型流感攻击的完全保护,通过短暂的早期病毒负荷减少以及随后的肺部病理学和肺屏障完整性的持续改善来预防发病和死亡率。
局限与证据强度
这些发现凸显了母乳来源的分泌型 IgA 作为一种可扩展的、针对粘膜的被动免疫策略的潜力,能够应对地方性和新出现的呼吸道病毒威胁。

Polyclonal breast milk–derived secretory IgA confers broad antiviral protection against multiple respiratory viruses

Authors: Yona Tugg, Jann Catherine Ang, Imran Ahmed, Rikin Mehta, Viraj P. Mane, Sam Afkhami, Matthew S. Miller
Original abstract (English)
Passive immunization is an important tool for protecting individuals with primary immune deficiencies from infectious diseases and offers a rapid response strategy for protecting vulnerable populations, particularly during the interval between pathogen emergence and vaccine deployment. Current approaches rely on intravenous delivery of IgG-enriched plasma or monoclonal antibodies, which limits accessibility, scalability, and effectiveness at mucosal surfaces, the primary entry point for respiratory viruses. Here, we tested LCTG-001, a polyclonal secretory IgA preparation derived from pooled human breast milk, to address these limitations. LCTG-001 demonstrated broad antiviral activity against clinically relevant respiratory viruses, including respiratory syncytial virus, adenovirus, and influenza A virus. LCTG-001 effectively recognized both group 1 and group 2 influenza A subtypes, including the highly pathogenic H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b, and engaged nonneutralizing effector mechanisms such as neuraminidase inhibition and neutrophil activation. When administered intranasally, LCTG-001 conferred complete protection against influenza A challenge in both immunocompetent and antibody-deficient murine models, preventing morbidity and mortality through a transient early reduction in viral burden followed by sustained improvements in lung pathology and lung barrier integrity. These findings highlight the potential of breast milk-derived secretory IgA as a scalable, mucosa-targeted passive immunization strategy capable of addressing both endemic and emerging respiratory viral threats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Passive immunization is an important tool for protecting individuals with primary immune deficiencies from infectious diseases and offers a rapid response strategy for protecting vulnerable populations, particularly during the interval between pathogen emergence and vaccine deployment.
Design and population
Current approaches rely on intravenous delivery of IgG-enriched plasma or monoclonal antibodies, which limits accessibility, scalability, and effectiveness at mucosal surfaces, the primary entry point for respiratory viruses.

沙特阿拉伯吉达市儿童流感疫苗接种率的父母知识、态度和行为调查:一项横断面研究

Parental Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices Toward Influenza Vaccine Uptake Among Children in Jeddah, Saudi Arabia: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Amjad Alharazi, Majid Alghamdi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感疫苗接种是重要的公共卫生问题,尤其是对儿童而言,他们感染风险较高且可能将病毒传播给他人。
设计与对象
在吉达初级医疗中心就诊的沙特父母中进行了一项横断面研究。至少有一名六个月或以上孩子的父母符合资格。
核心方法
使用经过验证的自填阿拉伯语问卷收集数据,评估知识、态度、意识、感知障碍和 疫苗 的吸收。使用描述性统计和卡方检验来检查关联性,并进行二元逻辑回归来确定 疫苗 摄取的独立预测因素。
主要结果
A total of 308 parents participated. Influenza vaccine uptake was reported by 57.5% of participants. Nearly all participants (97.7%) demonstrated good knowledge, but the knowledge score was not significantly associated with vaccine uptake (p = 0.399). Parental attitude was a significant independent predictor (OR = 1.090, 95% CI: 1.033-1.149, p = 0.001). Education level was also significantly associated with uptake (p = 0.015), with lower-educated parents showing higher odds of vaccination compared to postgraduate-educated parents. Exposure to awareness campaigns was significantly associated with higher uptake (p < 0.001).
解释与创新
尽管大多数父母对流感疫苗接种有良好的知识,但这并未转化为一致的疫苗接种行为。父母的态度和宣传运动的接触是疫苗接种的主要预测因素。
科研与公卫意义
公共卫生策略应从广泛的事实传播转向有针对性的、现场咨询,以解决对疫苗接种必要性和安全性的误解,从而提高沙特阿拉伯儿童的流感疫苗接种率。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Parental Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices Toward Influenza Vaccine Uptake Among Children in Jeddah, Saudi Arabia: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Amjad Alharazi, Majid Alghamdi
Original abstract (English)
Background: Seasonal influenza is a major public health concern, particularly among children who are at high risk of infection and can transmit the virus to others. Vaccination is the primary preventive measure. Parental knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) may influence vaccination decisions. Methods: A cross-sectional study was conducted among Saudi parents attending primary healthcare centers in Jeddah. Parents with at least one child aged six months or older were eligible. Data were collected using a validated self-administered Arabic questionnaire assessing knowledge, attitudes, awareness, perceived barriers, and vaccine uptake. Descriptive statistics and chi-square tests were used to examine associations, and binary logistic regression was performed to identify independent predictors of vaccine uptake. Results: A total of 308 parents participated. Influenza vaccine uptake was reported by 57.5% of participants. Nearly all participants (97.7%) demonstrated good knowledge, but the knowledge score was not significantly associated with vaccine uptake (p = 0.399). Parental attitude was a significant independent predictor (OR = 1.090, 95% CI: 1.033-1.149, p = 0.001). Education level was also significantly associated with uptake (p = 0.015), with lower-educated parents showing higher odds of vaccination compared to postgraduate-educated parents. Exposure to awareness campaigns was significantly associated with higher uptake (p < 0.001). The most reported barriers were the belief that vaccination was unnecessary (9.4%) and concerns about side effects (8.4%), both strongly associated with non-vaccination (p < 0.001). Conclusion: Although most parents had good knowledge about influenza vaccination, this did not translate into consistent vaccination behavior. Parental attitudes and awareness campaign exposure were the main predictors of vaccine uptake. Public health strategies should shift from broad factual dissemination to targeted, point-of-care counseling that addresses misconceptions about vaccine necessity and safety to improve influenza vaccination rates among children in Saudi Arabia.

急性呼吸道病原体变化:一项在中国杭州(2018–2025)进行的8年研究

Shifts in acute respiratory pathogen epidemiology: an 8-year surveillance study in Hangzhou, China (2018–2025)
作者： Wang Y, Huo L, Wang J, Jin X, Jiang L
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的分析杭州市2018—2025年急性呼吸道感染(ARTI)病例6种常见呼吸道病原体的流行病学特征,为临床诊断和治疗以及公共卫生防控提供依据。方法采用多中心回顾性研究。对检测结果呈阳性的病例进行甲型流感病毒(FluA)、乙型流感病毒(FluB)、呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)、腺病毒(ADV)、肺炎支原体(MP)、肺炎衣原体(CP)的检测,并按照流行阶段、年龄、性别、季节、合并感染情况进行统计分析。结果 81,974例患者中,共有33,350例检测结果呈阳性病原菌总体阳性率为40.68%。研究结果提示,需建立动态监测体系,对高发病率季节的重点人群和病原体实施有针对性的防控措施。关键词:急性呼吸道感染,流行病学特征,杭州,流感病毒,肺炎支原体,病原谱2.材料与方法2.1.研究人群本研究旨在系统地表征六种重点呼吸道感染的流行病学特征。杭州 ARTI 病例中的病原体——甲型流感病毒 (FluA)、乙型流感病毒 (FluB)、RSV、腺病毒 (ADV)、肺炎支原体 (MP) 和肺炎衣原体 (CP),并评估 COVID-19 大流行对当地病原体动态的长期影响。收集2018年1月至2025年12月杭州市7家医疗机构81974例ARTI病例的数据。MP (19.56%) 和 FluA (16.93%) 是最常见的病原体。每项检测的阳性率...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在系统地描述杭州市急性呼吸道感染(ARTI)病例中六种主要呼吸道病原体的流行病学特征,并评估COVID-19大流行对当地病原体动态的长期影响。
设计与对象
该研究是一项回顾性 监测 研究,于 2018 年至 2025 年在中国杭州进行,分析了 81,974 名 ARTI 患者。对病例进行了甲型流感病毒(FluA)、乙型流感病毒(FluB)、呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)、腺病毒(ADV)、肺炎支原体(MP)和肺炎衣原体(CP)检测。
核心方法
阳性标准遵循制造商针对每种检测的说明。计算合并感染率,并根据流行阶段、年龄、性别、季节、合并感染状况进行统计分析。
主要结果
81974例患者中,病原体检测呈阳性33350例,总体阳性率为40.68%。FluA、FluB和MP的阳性率在COVID-19大流行期间(2020-2022年)显着受到抑制,然后在大流行后时期(2023-2025年)急剧反弹。相比之下,RSV、ADV 和 CP 显示出持续的抑制作用,但绝对病例数比大流行前更高。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了呼吸道病原体流行病学的动态变化,FluA、FluB和MP在大流行后表现出“报复性反弹”,而RSV、ADV和CP仍然受到抑制,但绝对数量有所增加。这种模式凸显了大流行和季节性呼吸道病原体之间复杂的相互作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明,需要建立动态监测系统来跟踪病原体发病率的变化,并在发病率高发季节实施有针对性的预防和控制措施,特别是针对A型流感、B型流感和MP。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性设计,可能会引入偏差,并且观察到的阳性率变化可能受到公共卫生干预等外部因素的影响。由于这些限制,证据是中等的。

Shifts in acute respiratory pathogen epidemiology: an 8-year surveillance study in Hangzhou, China (2018–2025)

Authors: Wang Y, Huo L, Wang J, Jin X, Jiang L
Original abstract (English)
AbstractObjectivesTo analyze the epidemiological characteristics of six common respiratory pathogens in cases of Acute Respiratory Tract Infection (ARTI) in Hangzhou from 2018 to 2025, to provide a basis for clinical diagnosis and treatment, as well as public health prevention and control.MethodsA multicenter retrospective study was conducted. Cases with positive test results were analyzed for influenza A virus (FluA), influenza B virus (FluB), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), adenovirus (ADV), Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP), and Chlamydia pneumoniae (CP), and statistical analysis was performed according to the pandemic stage, age, sex, season, and co-infection status.ResultsAmong 81,974 patients, a total of 33,350 cases tested positive for pathogens, with an overall positive rate of 40.68%. The research results suggest that a dynamic monitoring system needs to be established, and targeted prevention and control measures should be implemented for key individuals and pathogens during high-incidence seasons.Keywords: Acute Respiratory Tract Infection, epidemiological characteristics, Hangzhou, influenza virus, Mycoplasma pneumoniae, pathogen spectrum 2.Materials and methods2.1.Study populationThis study aimed to systematically characterize the epidemiological features of six key respiratory pathogens—influenza A virus (FluA), influenza B virus (FluB), RSV, adenovirus (ADV), Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP), and Chlamydia pneumoniae (CP)—among ARTI cases in Hangzhou, and to evaluate the long-term impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on local pathogen dynamics. Data from 81,974 ARTI cases from seven medical institutions in Hangzhou City between January 2018 and December 2025 were collected. MP (19.56%) and FluA (16.93%) were the most prevalent pathogens. The positivity rates of each pa...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to systematically characterize the epidemiological features of six key respiratory pathogens among acute respiratory tract infection (ARTI) cases in Hangzhou and evaluate the long-term impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on local pathogen dynamics.
Design and population
The study was a retrospective surveillance study conducted in Hangzhou, China, from 2018 to 2025, analyzing 81,974 patients with ARTI. Cases were tested for influenza A virus (FluA), influenza B virus (FluB), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), adenovirus (ADV), Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP), and Chlamydia pneumoniae (CP).
Core methods
Positivity criteria followed the manufacturers' instructions for each respective assay. The co-infection rate was calculated, and statistical analysis was performed according to the pandemic stage, age, sex, season, and co-infection status.
Main results
Among 81,974 patients, 33,350 tested positive for pathogens, with an overall positivity rate of 40.68%. The positivity rates of FluA, FluB, and MP were significantly suppressed during the COVID-19 pandemic period (2020–2022) and then rebounded sharply in the post-pandemic period (2023–2025). In contrast, RSV, ADV, and CP showed persistent inhibition but had higher absolute case numbers than before the pandemic.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal a dynamic shift in the epidemiology of respiratory pathogens, with FluA, FluB, and MP exhibiting a "revenge rebound" after the pandemic, while RSV, ADV, and CP remained inhibited but increased in absolute numbers. This pattern highlights the complex interplay between the pandemic and seasonal respiratory pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results suggest the need for a dynamic monitoring system to track changes in pathogen prevalence and implement targeted prevention and control measures during high-incidence seasons, particularly for FluA, FluB, and MP.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a retrospective design, which may introduce bias, and the observed changes in positivity rates could be influenced by external factors such as public health interventions. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

问题与背景
该研究旨在探索半枝莲对抗甲型H1N1流感病毒的机制和活性化合物,满足了解其抗病毒药物特性和潜在治疗应用的需要。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 MDCK 细胞进行的体外测定和小鼠体内实验,后者分为对照组、模型组和 治疗 组。治疗组接受半枝莲提取物,而模型组则暴露于病毒。
核心方法
该研究使用酶联免疫吸附测定(ELISA)试剂盒测量细胞因子水平,使用MTT测定评估细胞毒性,并使用定量实时PCR(qRT-PCR)检测病毒基因表达。还采用免疫荧光测定和组织病理学检查来分析病毒蛋白表达和组织变化。
主要结果

解释与创新
研究结果表明,半枝莲可能通过抑制关键信号通路来调节 抗病毒药物 和炎症反应,为其对抗甲型 H1N1 流感病毒的潜在治疗用途提供了新的视角。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对半枝莲 抗病毒药物 活性机制的深入了解,这可以为未来针对流感 治疗 的天然化合物的研究提供信息,并支持靶向治疗的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖体外和体内模型而受到限制,这些模型可能无法完全代表人类流感动态。证据是中等的,因为它基于没有直接临床结果的实验​​结果。

Exploration of the mechanisms and active compounds of Scutellaria barbata D. Don against influenza A (H1N1) virus

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the mechanisms and active compounds of Scutellaria barbata D. Don against influenza A (H1N1) virus, addressing the need for understanding its antiviral properties and potential therapeutic applications.
Design and population
The study involved in vitro assays using MDCK cells and in vivo experiments on mice, with the latter divided into control, model, and treatment groups. The treatment group received Scutellaria barbata extract, while the model group was exposed to the virus.
Core methods
The study used enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) kits to measure cytokine levels, MTT assay to assess cytotoxicity, and quantitative real-time PCR (qRT-PCR) to detect viral gene expression. Immunofluorescence assays and histopathological examination were also employed to analyze viral protein expression and tissue changes.
Main results
In vitro assays demonstrated that Scutellaria barbata suppressed viral gene expression and reduced inflammatory cytokine levels. The treatment also showed effects on the expression of antiviral and inflammatory pathways in infected cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that Scutellaria barbata may modulate antiviral and inflammatory responses through suppression of key signaling pathways, offering a novel perspective on its potential therapeutic use against influenza A (H1N1) virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the mechanisms of Scutellaria barbata's antiviral activity, which could inform future research on natural compounds for influenza treatment and support the development of targeted therapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on in vitro and in vivo models, which may not fully represent human influenza dynamics. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on experimental findings without direct clinical outcomes.

小儿重症病毒性脑炎住院 死亡率 的临床特征及探索性因素

Clinical characteristics and exploratory factors associated with in-hospital mortality in pediatric severe viral encephalitis
作者： He P, Hu F, Wang F
摘要中文翻译摘要目的:描述儿童重症病毒性脑炎(SVE)的临床特征,明确与院内死亡率相关的探索性因素,为早期危险分层、减少SVE相关残疾和死亡率提供初步证据。方法对安庆市立医院儿科自1月以来确诊的75例病毒性脑炎患儿进行回顾性研究。2023年至2025年12月,其中轻症病例50例,SVE病例25例。收集并比较组间的人口统计学数据、临床表现和实验室参数(外周白细胞计数(WBC)、血清钠、脑脊液(CSF)白细胞和蛋白质水平、血清神经元特异性烯醇化酶(NSE)、脑电图(EEG)、神经影像学结果和格拉斯哥昏迷量表(GCS)评分)。然而,考虑到样本量小和单中心设计,这些发现是假设生成的,需要在更大的多中心前瞻性 队列研究 中进行验证。早期识别这些因素可能有助于临床风险评估。关键词:儿童、临床特征、探索性因素、院内死亡率、重症病毒性脑炎2.对象与方法2.1.研究对象所有病例均来源于2023年1月至2025年12月在安庆市立医院儿科诊断为病毒性脑炎的儿科住院患者。通过多重PCR进行病原检测和/或血清病毒 IgM 抗体检测。在轻度脑炎组中,50例中有14例(28%)被发现感染了特定病毒,其中肠道病毒(EV)是主要类型,占7例,其次是带状疱疹病毒...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定与住院 死亡率 儿童重症病毒性脑炎相关的临床特征和探索性因素,满足更好地了解这种情况的危险因素的需要。
设计与对象
该研究于2023年1月至2025年12月在安庆市立医院儿科进行,涉及25名确诊为重症病毒性脑炎(SVE)的儿童和50名轻度脑炎儿童。
核心方法
通过多重 PCR 和/或血清病毒 IgM 抗体检测进行病原体检测。采用单变量分析和 Firth 惩罚逻辑回归来探讨与住院 死亡率 相关的因素。血清 NSE 被建模为每 10 ng/mL 增量的连续变量。入院24小时内进行神经影像学和脑电图检查。
主要结果
在 25 名 SVE 儿童中,发烧(76%)、颅内压升高(68%)和癫痫发作(56%)很常见,其中 4 名患者死亡(16%)。SVE组住院时间明显更长,癫痫发作率更高,GCS评分更低,脑电图和神经影像异常比例更高,血清NSE升高(均P < 0.05)。
解释与创新
神经影像学异常、癫痫发作、脑电图异常、较低的 GCS 评分和较高的血清 NSE 与 SVE 组中的住院 死亡率 显着相关。该研究强调了这些因素在预测严重后果方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
及早认识这些因素可能有助于临床风险评估并改善患有严重病毒性脑炎的儿童的管理策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的选择偏倚而受到限制。由于该研究的观察性质和缺乏随机对照,证据是中等的。

Clinical characteristics and exploratory factors associated with in-hospital mortality in pediatric severe viral encephalitis

Authors: He P, Hu F, Wang F
Original abstract (English)
AbstractObjectiveThe aim of this study was to describe the clinical characteristics of severe viral encephalitis (SVE) in children and to identify exploratory factors associated with in-hospital mortality, thereby providing preliminary evidence to facilitate early risk stratification and reduce SVE-related disability and mortality.MethodsA retrospective study was conducted in 75 children diagnosed with viral encephalitis at the Department of Pediatrics, Anqing Municipal Hospital, from January 2023 to December 2025, including 50 mild cases and 25 SVE cases. Demographic data, clinical manifestations, and laboratory parameters (peripheral white blood cell count (WBC), serum sodium, cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) leukocyte and protein levels, serum neuron-specific enolase (NSE), electroencephalography (EEG), neuroimaging findings, and Glasgow Coma Scale (GCS) scores) were collected and compared between groups. However, given the small sample size and single-center design, these findings are hypothesis-generating and require validation in larger multi-center prospective cohorts. Early recognition of these factors may aid in clinical risk assessment.Keywords: children, clinical characteristics, exploratory factors, in-hospital mortality, severe viral encephalitis 2.Subjects and methods2.1.Study subjectsAll cases were derived from pediatric inpatients diagnosed with viral encephalitis at the Department of Pediatrics, Anqing Municipal Hospital from January 2023 to December 2025. Pathogen detection was conducted through multiplex PCR and/or serum viral IgM antibody testing. In the mild encephalitis group, 14 out of 50 cases (28%) were found to be infected with specific viruses, with enteroviruses (EVs) being the predominant type, accounting for 7 cases, followed by herpes zoster vir...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify clinical characteristics and exploratory factors associated with in-hospital mortality in pediatric severe viral encephalitis, addressing the need to better understand risk factors for this condition.
Design and population
The study was conducted at the Department of Pediatrics, Anqing Municipal Hospital, from January 2023 to December 2025, involving 25 children diagnosed with severe viral encephalitis (SVE) and 50 children with mild encephalitis.
Core methods
Pathogen detection was conducted through multiplex PCR and/or serum viral IgM antibody testing. Univariate analyses and Firth-penalized logistic regression were used to explore factors associated with in-hospital mortality. Serum NSE was modeled as a continuous variable per 10 ng/mL increment. Neuroimaging and EEG were performed within 24 h of admission.
Main results
Among the 25 children with SVE, fever (76%), signs of elevated intracranial pressure (68%), and seizures (56%) were common, with four patients dying (16%). The SVE group had significantly longer hospital stays, higher seizure rates, lower GCS scores, higher proportions of abnormal EEG and neuroimaging abnormalities, and elevated serum NSE (all P < 0.05).
Interpretation and novelty
Neuroimaging abnormalities, seizures, EEG abnormalities, lower GCS score, and higher serum NSE were significantly associated with in-hospital mortality in the SVE group. The study highlights the importance of these factors in predicting severe outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
Early recognition of these factors may aid in clinical risk assessment and improve management strategies for children with severe viral encephalitis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the lack of randomized control.

鉴定断奶时的免疫能力标记物以预测猪的长期 疫苗 反应和持久性

Identifying the immunocompetence markers at weaning to predict long-term vaccine response and persistence in pigs
作者： Sabuj, A. A. M., Courbariaux, M., Denis, C., Lemonnier, G., ESTELLE, J., Pinard-van der Laan, M.-H., Rogel-Gaillard, C., Blanc, F.
摘要中文翻译疫苗接种 是控制猪传染病的关键策略,但 疫苗 诱导的免疫力的程度和持久性因个体而异。我们研究了断奶时测量的免疫和生理特征是否可以预测 疫苗接种 后针对猪肺炎支原体 (MH) 和甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 的长期抗体 (Ab) 状态和抗体持久性,以及 疫苗 之间是否共享预测标记。两项 疫苗接种 试验在 381 头大白猪(MH,n=189;IAV,n=192)的 队列研究 中进行。动物在断奶时(28 天)接种疫苗,并在 21 天后接受加强免疫。使用特定测定阈值在 疫苗接种 (dpv) 后 118 天定义长期 Ab 状态,而 Ab 持久性定义为从 28 或 35 dpv 时的个体峰值反应到 118 dpv 的下降。断奶时测量的一系列免疫和生理特征被评估为候选预测因子。使用稀疏偏最小二乘判别分析 (sPLS-DA) 进行变量选择,然后使用 PLS-DA 分类模型。交叉验证的准确度范围为 64.7% 至 67.7%,表明预测性能中等。在 疫苗 中,共有八个预测因子用于长期抗体状态,十二个预测因子用于抗体持久性。血细胞比容、IgG、血小板计数和血小板比容 (PCT) 与 疫苗 的长期抗体状态呈阳性相关,而平均红细胞血红蛋白浓度 (MCHC) 和中性粒细胞百分比与阴性状态相关。就持久性而言,较高的维生素 D、-1 球蛋白浓度、血小板计数和 PCT 与 疫苗 的持久性反应一致相关,而 MCHC 和光谱吸光度特征与非持久性相关。这些发现表明,断奶时测量的早期免疫和生理特征与免疫能力特征相关,这些特征导致随后的长期 疫苗 抗体结果和反应性的变化。共享预测因子和 疫苗 或表型特异性预测因子的鉴定表明,疫苗 反应性反映了基线免疫能力的一般特征和特定背景的决定因素。

问题与背景
疫苗接种 是控制猪传染病的关键策略,但 疫苗 诱导的免疫力的程度和持久性因个体而异。
设计与对象
两项 疫苗接种 试验在 381 头大白猪(MH,n=189;IAV,n=192)的 队列研究 中进行。
核心方法
使用特定测定阈值在 疫苗接种 (dpv) 后 118 天定义长期 Ab 状态,而 Ab 持久性定义为从 28 或 35 dpv 时的个体峰值反应到 118 dpv 的下降。
主要结果
血细胞比容、IgG、血小板计数和血小板比容 (PCT) 与 疫苗 的长期抗体状态呈阳性相关,而平均红细胞血红蛋白浓度 (MCHC) 和中性粒细胞百分比与阴性状态相关。就持久性而言,较高的维生素 D、-1 球蛋白浓度、血小板计数和 PCT 与 疫苗 的持久性反应一致相关,而 MCHC 和光谱吸光度特征与非持久性相关。

这些发现表明,断奶时测量的早期免疫和生理特征与免疫能力特征相关,这些特征导致随后的长期 疫苗 抗体结果和反应性的变化。
科研与公卫意义

局限与证据强度
交叉验证的准确度范围为 64.7% 至 67.7%,表明预测性能中等。

Identifying the immunocompetence markers at weaning to predict long-term vaccine response and persistence in pigs

Authors: Sabuj, A. A. M., Courbariaux, M., Denis, C., Lemonnier, G., ESTELLE, J., Pinard-van der Laan, M.-H., Rogel-Gaillard, C., Blanc, F.
Original abstract (English)
Vaccination is a key strategy for controlling infectious disease in pigs, but the magnitude and persistence of vaccine-induced immunity vary among individuals. We investigated whether immune and physiological traits measured at weaning could predict long-term antibody (Ab) status and Ab persistence following vaccination against Mycoplasma hyopneumoniae (MH) and Influenza A virus (IAV), and whether predictive markers were shared between vaccines. Two vaccination trials were conducted in a cohort of 381 Large White pigs (MH, n=189; IAV, n=192). Animals were vaccinated at weaning (28 days of age) and received a booster 21 days later. Long-term Ab status was defined at 118 days post-vaccination (dpv) using assay-specific thresholds, while Ab persistence was defined as the decline from the individual peak response at 28 or 35 dpv to 118 dpv. A broad panel of immune and physiological traits measured at weaning was evaluated as candidate predictors. Sparse Partial Least Squares Discriminant Analysis (sPLS-DA) was used for variable selection, followed by PLS-DA classification models. Cross-validated accuracies ranged from 64.7% to 67.7%, indicating moderate predictive performance. Across vaccines, eight predictors were shared for long-term Ab status and twelve for Ab persistence. Hematocrit, IgG, platelet count, and plateletcrit (PCT) were associated with positive long-term Ab status for both vaccines, whereas mean corpuscular hemoglobin concentration (MCHC) and neutrophil percentage were associated with negative status. For persistence, higher vitamin D, -1 globulin concentration, platelet count, and PCT were consistently associated with persistent responses across vaccines, while MCHC and spectral absorbance traits were associated with non-persistence. These findings indicate that early-life immune and physiological traits measured at weaning are associated with immunocompetence traits that contribute to variation in subsequent long-term vaccine antibody outcomes and responsiveness. The identification of both shared and vaccine- or phenotype-specific predictors suggests that vaccine responsiveness reflects both general features of baseline immunocompetence and context-specific determinants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Vaccination is a key strategy for controlling infectious disease in pigs, but the magnitude and persistence of vaccine-induced immunity vary among individuals.

对密歇根州和香港流感低流行期间流感抗体减弱的前瞻性比较 队列研究 分析

A prospective, comparative cohort analysis of influenza antibody waning in Michigan and Hong Kong during periods of low influenza circulation
作者： Yang, Y., Smith, M., Ho, F., Truscon, R., Leung, N. H. L., Touyon, L., Fitzsimmons, W. J., Callear, A., Godonou, E.-T., Blair, C. N.
摘要中文翻译背景:COVID-19 大流行期间流感 传播 的减少引发了人们对人群免疫力减弱的担忧,这可能导致随后的流通量激增。我们比较了密歇根州和香港的纵向流感抗体滴度,这两个地区在 COVID-19 大流行期间流感 传播 有所降低。方法:在两项前瞻性 队列研究 研究(密歇根州 HIVE;香港 EPI-HK)中,我们分析了 2020 年至 2023 年从未感染流感或 疫苗接种 的参与者收集的纵向血清样本。使用针对 疫苗 菌株的血凝抑制测定 (HAI) 和微量中和 (MN) 测试血清。使用针对年龄和基线滴度进行调整的多变量幂律模型来估计滴度的两年倍数变化。结果:我们分析了 57 名 HIVE 参与者的 173 份血清和 60 名 EPI-HK 参与者的 259 份血清。HIVE 中初始 HAI 几何平均滴度为 12.3-123.4,EPI-HK 为 6.3-40.9。在 HIVE 基线滴度 >1:40 的人群中,倍数变化估计表明 H1N1 毒株减弱,包括 A/布里斯班 (0.70 [95% CI: 0.56,0.86])、A/爱达荷 (0.40 [0.32,0.50]) 和 A/密歇根 (0.43 [0.34,0.52]),而 H3N2 和乙型流感表现出最小的减弱或没有减弱。在 EPI-HK 中,倍数变化估计显示了 H1N1 病毒株的异质模式,而选定的 H3N2 病毒株的变化则呈减弱趋势,包括 A/柬埔寨 (0.44 [0.29,0.65]) 和 A/新加坡 (0.48 [0.33,0.69])。MN 结果与 HAI 结果一致。结论:大流行期间的流感抗体反应表现出异质性、毒株特异性变化,而不是统一下降。这些发现表明,尽管病毒循环减少,但许多毒株的群体免疫力仍得以维持,但特定毒株的免疫力减弱,且因群体而异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
COVID-19 大流行期间流感 传播 的减少引发了人们对人群免疫力减弱的担忧,这可能导致随后的流通量激增。我们比较了密歇根州和香港的纵向流感抗体滴度,这两个地区在 COVID-19 大流行期间流感 传播 有所降低。
设计与对象
在两项前瞻性 队列研究 研究(密歇根州 HIVE;香港 EPI-HK)中,我们分析了 2020 年至 2023 年从未感染流感或 疫苗接种 的参与者收集的纵向血清样本。
核心方法
使用针对 疫苗 菌株的血凝抑制测定 (HAI) 和微量中和 (MN) 测试血清。使用针对年龄和基线滴度进行调整的多变量幂律模型来估计滴度的两年倍数变化。
主要结果
我们分析了 57 名 HIVE 参与者的 173 份血清和 60 名 EPI-HK 参与者的 259 份血清。HIVE 中初始 HAI 几何平均滴度为 12.3-123.4,EPI-HK 为 6.3-40.9。在 HIVE 基线滴度 >1:40 的人群中,倍数变化估计表明 H1N1 毒株减弱,包括 A/布里斯班 (0.70 [95% CI: 0.56,0.86])、A/爱达荷 (0.40 [0.32,0.50]) 和 A/密歇根 (0.43 [0.34,0.52]),而 H3N2 和乙型流感表现出最小的减弱或没有减弱。在 EPI-HK 中,倍数变化估计显示了 H1N1 病毒株的异质模式,而选定的 H3N2 病毒株的变化则呈减弱趋势,包括 A/柬埔寨 (0.44 [0.29,0.65]) 和 A/新加坡 (0.48 [0.33,0.69])。MN 结果与 HAI 结果一致。

这些发现表明,尽管病毒循环减少,但许多毒株的群体免疫力仍得以维持,但特定毒株的免疫力减弱,且因群体而异。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过强调抗体减弱的毒株特异性变异来为 监测 策略和 疫苗 开发提供信息,这可能会影响未来的流感 暴发 和公共卫生干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖于未感染流感或 疫苗接种 的参与者的血清样本而受到限制,并且由于 队列研究 研究的观察性质和潜在的混杂因素,证据是中等的。

A prospective, comparative cohort analysis of influenza antibody waning in Michigan and Hong Kong during periods of low influenza circulation

Authors: Yang, Y., Smith, M., Ho, F., Truscon, R., Leung, N. H. L., Touyon, L., Fitzsimmons, W. J., Callear, A., Godonou, E.-T., Blair, C. N.
Original abstract (English)
Background: Reduced influenza transmission during the COVID-19 pandemic prompted concern about waning of population immunity that could lead to subsequent surges in circulation. We compared longitudinal influenza antibody titers in Michigan and Hong Kong, two regions with reduced influenza transmission during the COVID-19 pandemic. Methods: In two prospective cohort studies (HIVE, Michigan; EPI-HK, Hong Kong), we analyzed longitudinal serum samples collected from 2020 through 2023 from participants without influenza infection or vaccination. Sera were tested using hemagglutination inhibition assays (HAI) and microneutralization (MN) against vaccine strains. Two-year fold changes in titers were estimated using multivariable power-law models adjusted for age and baseline titer. Results: We analyzed 173 sera from 57 HIVE participants and 259 sera from 60 EPI-HK participants. Initial HAI geometric mean titers ranged from 12.3-123.4 in HIVE and 6.3-40.9 in EPI-HK. Among those with >1:40 baseline titers in HIVE, fold-change estimates indicated waning for H1N1 strains, including A/Brisbane (0.70 [95% CI: 0.56,0.86]), A/Idaho (0.40 [0.32,0.50]), and A/Michigan (0.43 [0.34,0.52]), while H3N2 and influenza B showed minimal or no waning. In EPI-HK, fold-change estimates demonstrated heterogeneous patterns by strain for H1N1, and waning for selected H3N2 strains, including A/Cambodia (0.44 [0.29,0.65]) and A/Singapore (0.48 [0.33,0.69]). MN results were consistent with HAI findings. Conclusion: Influenza antibody responses during the pandemic showed heterogeneous, strain-specific changes rather than uniform decline. These findings suggest that population immunity was maintained for many strains despite reduced viral circulation, with waning occurring in particular strains and varying by population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Reduced influenza transmission during the COVID-19 pandemic prompted concern about waning of population immunity that could lead to subsequent surges in circulation. We compared longitudinal influenza antibody titers in Michigan and Hong Kong, two regions with reduced influenza transmission during the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
In two prospective cohort studies (HIVE, Michigan; EPI-HK, Hong Kong), we analyzed longitudinal serum samples collected from 2020 through 2023 from participants without influenza infection or vaccination.
Core methods
Sera were tested using hemagglutination inhibition assays (HAI) and microneutralization (MN) against vaccine strains. Two-year fold changes in titers were estimated using multivariable power-law models adjusted for age and baseline titer.
Main results
We analyzed 173 sera from 57 HIVE participants and 259 sera from 60 EPI-HK participants. Initial HAI geometric mean titers ranged from 12.3-123.4 in HIVE and 6.3-40.9 in EPI-HK. Among those with >1:40 baseline titers in HIVE, fold-change estimates indicated waning for H1N1 strains, including A/Brisbane (0.70 [95% CI: 0.56,0.86]), A/Idaho (0.40 [0.32,0.50]), and A/Michigan (0.43 [0.34,0.52]), while H3N2 and influenza B showed minimal or no waning. In EPI-HK, fold-change estimates demonstrated heterogeneous patterns by strain for H1N1, and waning for selected H3N2 strains, including A/Cambodia (0.44 [0.29,0.65]) and A/Singapore (0.48 [0.33,0.69]). MN results were consistent with HAI findings.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that population immunity was maintained for many strains despite reduced viral circulation, with waning occurring in particular strains and varying by population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance strategies and vaccine development by highlighting strain-specific variations in antibody waning, which may affect future influenza outbreaks and public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on serum samples from participants without influenza infection or vaccination, and the evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the cohort studies and potential confounding factors.
